2026-07-06 02:41:00 HR1 (i18n mechanism): Built the bilingual EN/HR i18n LAYER for the 6MB single-file user portal (user-portal/frontend/index.html) — this is the ESTABLISHED MECHANISM later clusters MUST USE, do not reinvent. MECHANISM = global window.MedXI18n injected right after <body> (self-contained IIFE, defensive, never breaks the app). Dictionary MedXI18n._DICT maps a stable KEY -> {en,hr}, grouped by section with comment headers (nav.*, a11y.*, account.*, projects.*, notif.*, splash.*, footer.*). RULES for future agents (also in the big comment block above the script): (1) STATIC HTML -> put data-i18n="key" on the element that OWNS the text; the DOM pass MedXI18n.apply() replaces only the element's FIRST significant TEXT NODE, so sibling icons/badges are preserved (proven: Rewards row kept its "410" chip) — NO span-wrapping needed. Attributes use data-i18n-placeholder / data-i18n-aria-label / data-i18n-title="key"; rare markup strings use data-i18n-html. (2) JS-GENERATED strings (template literals, toasts, modals) -> wrap with MedXI18n.t('key'[, {vars}]); {name} interpolation supported; convenience global t() also exposed. (3) MISSING key OR missing hr -> SILENT ENGLISH FALLBACK (a data-i18n element whose key is unknown is left untouched, keeping its baked English). (4) DATES -> use MedXI18n.formatDate(dateish, opts) / MedXI18n.dateLocale() ('hr-HR'|'en-US') instead of hard-coded 'en-US'. (5) ALWAYS ship en+hr in the SAME pass (owner rule); NEVER translate proper names, Med&X, Plexus, program brands (Accelerator, Building Bridges/"Bridges"), emails, legal-entity strings; Croatian formal register (Vi), correct diacritics, no semicolons. ## LinkedIn badge + public membership verification (final batch cluster)

Owner-approved. User-portal only. No schema change (HMAC-derived token), so the SCHEMA-MIRROR block stays byte-identical and admin server.js was not touched.

**Backend (user-portal/backend/server.js):**
- `verifyBadgeToken(userId)` / `resolveVerifyBadgeToken(token)` — stable signed slug per member = `base64url(userId).hmac_sha256(JWT_SECRET,'medx-verify-badge:'+userId)[:24]`, constant-time compared. No storage, no schema change, revocation-safe.
- `renderVerifyPage(state)` — premium public page (dark navy + gold seal, Cormorant/Inter), bilingual EN default with live HR toggle (formal Vi register, correct diacritics), meta noindex.
- `GET /verify/:token` (public, registered BEFORE express.static so it beats the SPA) — shows ONLY name, member-since year, membership class (member_type label, hidden for quiet profiles), and the Med&X brand. Never email, never points, never the rewards tier. Optional photo only when is_public_profile=1. Falls back to neutral "No active record" for: unresolvable/forged token, user not found, deleted_at set (anonymized), or standing != good_standing (lapsed/pending). Cache-Control no-store.
- `GET /api/member/verify-link` (auth) — returns the member's own token + path + name + issue_year + cert_name + organization. Available to ALL members incl. quiet (professional credential, not gamification).

**Frontend (user-portal/frontend/index.html, via python heredoc):**

2026-07-06 ACCELERATOR SUBMISSION-AND-SCORING PIPELINE — applicant side (user portal) — BUILT + E2E VERIFIED:
Owner-priority cluster: a clean, window-gated apply journey for the Med&X Accelerator, architected generic so it can later serve conference-abstract review (generic internals, accelerator-specific surface). Bilingual EN + HR throughout (formal Vi), no semicolons in user-facing copy, no word 'honest'.

ARCHITECTURE / NAMING DECISION: the task named the store `accelerator_applications`, but that table ALREADY EXISTS with an incompatible research-placement schema (institutions/interviews/ranking). A guarded `CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS accelerator_applications (...)` would be a silent no-op and inserts of cycle/payload_json would throw "no such column", and reusing it would pollute the legacy /my-applications, ranking and evaluate flows. So I created a NEW generic table `submission_pipeline` (id, user_id, track DEFAULT 'accelerator', cycle, payload_json, status, submitted_at, decision_at, created_at, updated_at) — the `track` discriminator is the generic hook for abstract review later. The legacy accelerator flow is left 100% untouched.

BACKEND (both server.js — SHARED tables added IDENTICALLY after the SCHEMA-MIRROR:END marker, check-schema-sync.sh exit 0):
- `submission_pipeline` (generic intake+review store) and `intake_windows` (admin-configurable open/close per track+cycle, UNIQUE(track,cycle)). Seeded iw_accelerator_2026 opens 2026-11-01, closes NULL (admin-set later) via INSERT OR IGNORE (never clobbers admin edits).
- User-portal routes (applicant surface): GET /api/accelerator/intake (public window+state before|open|closed), GET /api/accelerator/intake/mine (auth, status card + resume draft), POST /api/accelerator/intake/draft (auth, autosave, gated to open window, team capped 5, payload size-guarded), POST /api/accelerator/intake/:id/submit (auth, server-side required-field validation, flips to submitted, ONE confirmation email), POST /api/accelerator/intake/:id/withdraw (auth, only from submitted/under_review), PUT /api/accelerator/intake/window (auth+adminOnly, hosted here mirroring the existing admin-token routes like /api/accelerator/years).
- Confirmation email is bilingual by member locale, staged into the ONE approval outbox (scheduled_emails, source_engine 'accelerator-intake', template 'acc_submit') via buildEmailTemplate, idempotent per submission via drip_log kind acc_submit:<id>. Enqueue-only; the dev drainer sends.
- Optional single PDF reuses the existing upload boundary (POST /api/upload/accelerator, 10MB cap, Cloudinary/local, graceful 503 in prod without persistent storage) — no new upload path.

FRONTEND (user-portal/frontend/index.html, all edits via python3 heredoc with assert count==1):

## Med&X Accelerator — Submissions Review & Scoring Engine (admin) — 2026-07-06

Built the admin-side submission-and-scoring pipeline as a GENERIC "submissions review" engine (domain-neutral internals so conference-abstract review plugs in later) surfaced as **Accelerator** now. All work in `admin-portal` only; the user portal was not touched, and `scripts/check-schema-sync.sh` stays green (435-line mirror block unchanged).

### Schema (admin-only, guarded, OUTSIDE the SCHEMA-MIRROR block — admin server.js only)
- `review_rubrics` — one row per review **cycle** (`cycle_key`). Holds the admin-editable `criteria` (JSON: key/name/name_hr/description/description_hr/weight), `scale_min/max` (1..5), `reviewers_per_submission`, the per-cycle `blind_review` toggle, and `status` (draft|active). Seeded `accelerator-2026` as a DRAFT with the owner's 5 criteria (25/20/20/25/10 = 100). The owner approves it by editing — nothing hardcoded.
- `review_assignments` — links a reviewer (`reviewer_kind` admin→users.id | external→review_reviewers.id) to a `submission_id` (generic `submission_source`, `accelerator_applications` now). One-click recusal sets status='recused'.
- `review_scores` — one weighted scorecard per assignment. `scores` JSON map, `weighted_total` recomputed on every save, `private_comments` (committee-only) + `feedback_to_applicant`.
- `review_reviewers` — invited EXTERNAL reviewer accounts (limited, magic-link, `access_token`). Kept OUT of the members `users` table by design (scanner-staff spirit) so they never surface in the member portal.

### Backend (admin server.js)
Generic helpers: submission resolver (`accelerator_applications` → normalized, blind-aware), blind sanitizer (drops identity fields + institution + rewrites document filenames), weighted-total (`Σ score·weight / Σ weight`), shared scorecard upsert. Routes: rubric GET/PUT (live weight-sum validation — a cycle can only go 'active' when weights = 100), blind toggle, reviewers list + external add/invite/toggle/regenerate, submissions overview, single/bulk (round-robin) assignment, admin reviewer dashboard + scorecard + recuse, progress, and the external magic-link surface `/api/review-access/:token/*`. Reviewer invite goes through the approval outbox (`scheduled_emails` pending_approval, source_engine='review-invite') with `drip_log` idempotency (one invite/reviewer/day) — bilingual EN+HR email.

### Frontend
- Admin: new **Review** tab in the Accelerator section (`ReviewEngine` self-contained module in `admin-portal/frontend/index.html`, edited via python3 heredoc). Sub-tabs: Rubric (editable, live weight badge), Reviewers (internal + external + invite/copy-link), Assignments (per-submission chips + manual assign + round-robin auto-assign), My reviews (scorecard modal with live weighted total + recuse), Progress (fully-scored + per-reviewer table).
- External: standalone bilingual (EN/HR, formal Vi) magic-link workspace at `/review?token=…` — shows ONLY the reviewer's assignments, blind-aware, scorecard with save-draft/submit/recuse. No admin bundle.

### Verified (desktop 1440×900 + mobile 390×844, screenshots read then deleted)
- Rubric edit + weight-sum validation: activating at sum 90 rejected; sum 100 accepted.
- Round-robin auto-assign: 10 assignments, 2/sub across a 4-reviewer pool, balanced.
- Scored TWO applications from TWO reviewer sessions — external (4.4) and admin (4.1); both weighted totals **hand-verified against the DB** (MATCH).
- Blind toggle ON: names, institution AND the PDF filename (`Ivana_Brkic_CV_2026.pdf` → `Submission-APP-18D88FD8--cv.pdf`) all hidden; leak-check for the surname across the whole payload = false. Applies to the external view AND the admin reviewer scorecard.
- Recusal: status→recused, score row deleted, excluded from progress denominator.
- External token scoping: cannot open/score a non-assigned submission (403); invalid token (403).
- Invite queues to outbox once, idempotent on repeat; HR labels + criteria render on the external page.

Notes: demo review activity (assignments + 2 scorecards + 1 queued outbox invite to a demo reviewer) left in place to show the engine live. `node --check` clean, schema-sync clean, no git commits.

## Med&X Accelerator — A3: Decisions, AI decision letters, funnel, pulse + applicant surface — 2026-07-06

Cluster A3 closes the Accelerator loop into ONE coherent flow: apply (Nov 2026) -> assign -> score -> decide -> letter. Admin work is in `admin-portal`; the applicant reveal is a small `user-portal` change; both stay outside the SCHEMA-MIRROR block (A3 adds NO new tables, so `check-schema-sync.sh` stays green at 435 lines).

### STEP 0 — integration check (the A1<->A2 seam)
The A2 log recorded the resolver keyed on `submission_source='accelerator_applications'` (the LEGACY research-placement table), while A1 writes intake to `submission_pipeline` (track 'accelerator'). On inspection A2 had already generalized the resolver (`reviewListFromSource`/`reviewResolveFromSource` dispatch on the cycle's `source` column, with a `submission_pipeline` branch that normalizes payload_json, and a blind sanitizer that strips applicant + team-member names, institution and rewrites document filenames) and shipped a boot migration that repoints the `accelerator-2026` rubric `source` to `submission_pipeline`. The one gap was operational: the running admin process predated that migration so the live rubric still read `accelerator_applications`. Restarting applied the migration (verified `source=submission_pipeline`), and the resolver now reads the intake store. Seeded 3 realistic SUBMITTED applications in `submission_pipeline` (e2e.applicant.{a3,b3,c3}, projects NeuroSpark / GutGuard / RetinaAtlas) as evidence and to exercise the whole chain end to end. No resolver code change was needed beyond confirming and reusing it.

### Backend (admin server.js, all after SCHEMA-MIRROR:END)
- Decisions board data: `GET /api/admin/review/decisions` — every reviewable submission ranked by the mean weighted_total across SUBMITTED scorecards, with per-reviewer score chips, score spread (max-min), scoring-completeness (submitted >= active assignments), current decision + decision_at, and letter state.
- Decision action: `POST /api/admin/review/decisions/:id` writes `submission_pipeline.status` ('accepted'|'waitlisted'|'declined') + decision_at, or reverts to 'under_review'. Guard: incomplete scoring returns 409 needs_confirm unless `confirm_incomplete`. Lock: once the letter is approved (scheduled) or sent the decision is frozen (423). Revert cancels a still-pending letter and clears its drip marker so nothing stale is left.
- AI decision letters: `.../letter/preview` builds a warm bilingual EN+HR letter as editable plain text (next steps for accepts, the waiting-list process, a gracious close for declines) via a deterministic high-quality template, enriched by `aiDraft` only when a real ANTHROPIC key is present (mock never used as the body). `.../letter/queue` stages ONE pending_approval row in the shared outbox (source_engine 'accelerator-decision', template 'acc_decision', batch `acc-decision-<id>`), idempotent via drip_log 'acc_decision:<id>'. `.../letter/requeue` clears the marker + cancels a still-pending row (blocked once approved/sent). `.../letters/batch` queues every decided submission that lacks a letter. private_comments NEVER enter a letter (only feedback_to_applicant is offered, as an optional insert). Nothing auto-sends — the owner approves the batch in the existing outbox.
- Funnel: `GET /api/admin/review/funnel` — drafts -> submitted -> assigned -> fully scored -> decided (accepted/waitlisted/declined) -> letters sent, all live counts.
- Weekly pulse: added a live "Med&X Accelerator" section (submitted this week / awaiting review / decided this week) to `assemblePulseModel` + `renderPulseHtml`, additive next to the Forum block.

### Backend (user server.js) — reveal rule
`GET /api/accelerator/intake/mine` now masks a recorded decision to 'under_review' UNTIL its decision letter reaches status 'sent' in the shared outbox (joined server-side, not client-guessed) so the applicant never sees the outcome in the portal before the email reaches them.

### Frontend
- Admin ReviewEngine: new "Decisions" sub-tab in the Review tab — ranked cards (rank, applicant, mean, spread, completeness, per-reviewer chips), Accept/Waitlist/Decline with active + locked states, per-row letter status + Draft/View + Revert, "Draft letters for all decided", and an editable bilingual letter compose modal (subject + body, "Add reviewer notes" optional insert, Queue in outbox, discard-and-edit when queued, read-only when locked/sent).
- Admin: accelerator funnel strip at the top of the Accelerator section (forum-funnel styling), rendered on section show and refreshed after decisions/letters.
- User: AcceleratorIntake status card gains warm, distinct bilingual copy for accepted/waitlisted/declined (formal Vi, correct diacritics) via three new MedXI18n keys.
- Handbook: new numbered section 12 "Run the Med&X Accelerator" + TOC entry (section-11 precedent, no renumbering) — the yearly cycle in plain language (window, approve rubric by editing, invite reviewers, auto-assign, monitor, decide, approve letters), no semicolons.

### Verified E2E (real servers :3001/:3002, headless harness desktop 1440 + mobile 390, DB assertions)
- Seed -> assign 2 reviewers -> score both -> ranked NeuroSpark 4.5 > RetinaAtlas 4.125 > GutGuard 3.0; the top mean HAND-VERIFIED against the DB ((5.0+4.0)/2 = 4.5, spread 1.0).
- Incomplete-scoring guard returns 409 needs_confirm; decide with confirm proceeds.
- Decide all three; queue NeuroSpark letter once (2nd call queued:false via drip_log); approve the batch in the outbox; the drainer flips it to 'sent'; after approval the decision is locked (423 on change).
- Portal reveal: NeuroSpark (letter sent) flips to Accepted with the warm EN + HR copy; RetinaAtlas (waitlisted) and GutGuard (declined) stay masked at "Under review" until their letters send.
- Funnel API == SQL (drafts 0 / submitted 3 / assigned 3 / fully scored 3 / decided 3 = 1+1+1 / letters sent 1). Pulse email section shows live numbers (submitted 3 / awaiting 0 / decided 3). Handbook section 12 renders desktop + mobile, TOC updated.

## 2026-07-06 — Admin scanner CAPTURE LAYER upgrade (BarcodeDetector + torch + continuous focus)

Scope: `admin-portal/frontend/index.html` ONLY. No server.js, no schema, no user-portal edits. FROZEN validation flow (`/api/admin/checkin/verify`, QR payload format, QR generation) untouched — only the camera/decode capture layer changed.

What changed
- New shared `QRCapture` capture-layer helper (window.QRCapture) used by BOTH admin scanners (the global Quick Check-in FAB scanner and the EventCheckin modal):
  - `decode(canvas, ctx, opts)` — BarcodeDetector-first (feature-detected `window.BarcodeDetector` + `getSupportedFormats()` includes `qr_code`, cached), near-instant hardware decode; falls back to the vendored jsQR path UNCHANGED on Safari/older or on a per-frame hardware hiccup. `{forceJsqr:true}` bypasses BarcodeDetector (guaranteed Safari path + test hook). Returns the raw decoded string, which feeds the existing frozen validation branches verbatim.
  - Torch: `hasTorch()/setTorch()` via `MediaStreamTrack.getCapabilities().torch` + `applyConstraints({advanced:[{torch}]})`; `applyContinuousFocus()` requests `focusMode:'continuous'` where supported.
- Both scan loops (`App._scanGlobalFrame`, `EventCheckin.scanFrame`) rewritten to be async around `QRCapture.decode`; the global decode-handling branching was extracted verbatim into `App._onGlobalDecoded` (member ticket / profile lookup / plain check-in) with byte-identical behavior — only the decode SOURCE changed.
- Flashlight button added to both scanner UIs (`#globalQRTorch`, `#eventCheckinTorch`): hidden when the camera exposes no torch capability; visible OFF (dark) vs ON (gold, aria-pressed, glow) forced with inline `!important` to beat the theme's maroon button gradient. Admin surface = EN only (no i18n needed).
- Camera start now applies continuous autofocus where available.
- Offline guest-list cache + enrich call behavior left exactly as-is.

Verification (headless harness /tmp/medx-pw)
- Unit-drove the decode dispatch on a known QR image: forced-jsQR, REAL platform BarcodeDetector (available in harness Chromium), and a mocked BarcodeDetector branch all returned the IDENTICAL payload string; forced-jsQR still bypasses BarcodeDetector while one is present.
- Validation-call parity: feeding each path's payload into the frozen `verifyCode` produced a byte-identical POST to `/api/admin/checkin/verify` (same URL + body).
- Torch button states rendered desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844: hidden (no capability) / OFF (dark) / ON (gold) on both scanners.
- Existing offline harness (scanner_offline.js) still caches the roster (47), queues offline, matches the cached guest, and flushes on reconnect — unchanged.
- Zero app-origin console/page errors (one environmental `fonts.gstatic.com` woff2 failure from the no-network sandbox only).
- `node --check` on the full App/QRCapture/EventCheckin script block: OK. `scripts/check-schema-sync.sh`: exit 0.
- DB cleanup: reset the one `petra.uitest@example.com` check-in flag flipped by the offline reconnect-flush test back to checked_in=0. No other test rows created (e2e.* accelerator rows untouched).

## 2026-07-06 — Push-notification CLIENT loop + app-icon badge (user-portal)

Wired the missing web-push CLIENT side (server side — VAPID, /api/push/vapid-key, /api/push/subscribe, /api/push/unsubscribe, push_subscriptions, push_outbox drainer, SW push handler — already existed and was dormant because the frontend had ZERO pushManager.subscribe calls). No server.js changes, no schema changes.

Files touched (user-portal ONLY):
- user-portal/frontend/index.html — new `MedXPush` module + wiring (12 python-heredoc unique-string edits, each count==1).
- user-portal/frontend/sw.js — CACHE_NAME v7→v8, JSON push payload parse, focus-existing-tab notificationclick, and a `pushsubscriptionchange` re-subscribe handler.

What `MedXPush` does:
- VAPID key: reuses the existing `GET /api/push/vapid-key` (no new endpoint added).
- subscribe(): permission prompt ONLY on explicit user action -> pushManager.subscribe(userVisibleOnly, applicationServerKey) -> POST /api/push/subscribe with the member token. Sets `medx_push_enabled`.
- unsubscribe(): sub.unsubscribe() + DELETE /api/push/unsubscribe (by endpoint).
- Permission denied -> respectful copy, no re-prompt, no nagging.
- Expired/changed subs -> SW `pushsubscriptionchange` re-subscribes and postMessages the page to persist with auth; page also runs `ensureSubscribed()` on load (covers the app-closed case).
- App badge: navigator.setAppBadge/clearAppBadge mirrored to the unread count inside NotificationSystem.render() (set on change, cleared at 0 and on logout). Guarded for unsupported browsers.

Enable moments (never on first paint):
- The old first-paint 5s auto-banner was removed (checkPermissionPrompt now only reconciles the toggle).
- A tasteful bilingual banner is offered ONCE right after onboarding completes (WelcomeTour.complete -> MedXPush.offerAfterOnboarding).
- A proper toggle in Settings > Notifications ("Notifications on this device") subscribes/unsubscribes and reflects live state (on / off / blocked / unsupported / iOS-install).
- Bilingual EN+HR via MedXI18n.extend (push.* + set.push*), formal Vi, correct diacritics, live re-render on medx:localechange. No semicolons in user copy. Word "honest" not used.

iOS PWA reality (documented per requirement): iOS/iPadOS Safari only delivers Web Push from a PWA that has been Added to the Home Screen and opened FROM the Home Screen, and only on an explicit user gesture. `iosNeedsInstall()` = isIOS && !isStandalone. When true, the code shows guidance copy ("add Med&X to your Home Screen first, then open it from there") instead of a broken permission prompt — in the Settings toggle, in the onboarding banner, and in requestPermission. Once installed + opened standalone, the normal explicit-tap subscribe path runs.

Quiet-profile rule: push is a neutral utility, NOT a points/gamification surface, so it is correctly ungated. Quiet (gala/forum-only) members never run the onboarding tour, so they never see the post-onboarding banner, but they can enable push from Settings > Notifications. No quiet gating added or needed.

Verification (headless Chromium, /tmp/medx-pw harness, desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844):
- Client subscribe path proven END TO END against the live server: MedXPush.subscribe() -> real POST /api/push/subscribe -> real row in push_subscriptions (user_id = tester). Headless Chromium disables the Notifications API and cannot reach FCM, so only the browser push primitive (pushManager.subscribe) + Notification.permission were shimmed; the VAPID fetch, the authed POST, the DB write, localStorage flag were all real.
- Unsubscribe path: real DELETE /api/push/unsubscribe.
- App badge: NotificationSystem.render() at unread=4 -> setAppBadge(4); at unread=0 -> clearAppBadge (BADGE_CALLS ["set:4","clear"]).
- SERVER send attempt proven: seeded a real crypto subscription pointing at a local HTTPS mock, enqueued a push_outbox row; the live server's 45s drainPushOutbox delivered a real aes128gcm-encrypted POST (TTL 2419200, 199-byte body), marked the outbox row sent_count=1, and logged `[Push] Broadcast "E2E push send test" to 1 device(s)` with no server error. (Real device delivery via FCM cannot be proven headless/offline.)
- Bilingual: HR live re-render verified (card "Push obavijesti", row "Obavijesti na ovom uređaju", banner "Ostanite u tijeku" / "Uključi" / "Ne sada").
- Zero app-origin console/page errors from the feature (the only console line is a pre-existing anonymous-load `401 GET /api/project-status`, present on baseline before this change; PAGE_ERRORS []).
- node --check sw.js: OK. server.js untouched (node --check OK). scripts/check-schema-sync.sh: exit 0.
- DB cleanup: all E2E push_subscriptions + push_outbox test rows deleted (0 remain). Accelerator e2e.* rows untouched. Screenshots read then deleted.

OWNER / prod env vars needed on Render (user-portal service) to make push LIVE:
- VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY, VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY (generate once with `node -e "console.log(require('web-push').generateVAPIDKeys())"`; keep the private key secret), VAPID_SUBJECT (e.g. mailto:accelerator@medx.hr). Without these the server logs "[Push] VAPID keys not set" and everything degrades gracefully (client shows nothing broken; getVapidKey returns '' and the toggle simply can't enable). NOTE: the local dev shared .env already carries dev VAPID keys — prod MUST use its own dedicated keys, and the SAME public key must be what /api/push/vapid-key serves on prod. Admin portal needs NO VAPID (it only enqueues to push_outbox; the user portal drains + sends).

## 2026-07-06 — Session star ratings + Top rated (USER portal)

Built one-tap 1-5 star ratings on Talk Library cards plus a "Top rated" sort/section. User-portal only (`user-portal/`); admin-portal untouched.

### Backend (user-portal/backend/server.js)
- New USER-OWNED table `talk_ratings` (id, user_id, talk_id, rating 1-5, created_at, updated_at, UNIQUE(user_id, talk_id)) via guarded CREATE placed AFTER the SCHEMA-MIRROR:END marker (next to the talks duration/is_sample ALTERs). NOT mirrored to admin — admin never reads/writes it. Plus indexes on talk_id and user_id.
- `GET /api/talks` augmented: each row now carries `my_rating` (0 when unrated), `rating_count` (always), and `rating_avg` — avg is emitted ONLY when count >= 3 (single-vote boards suppressed server-side, not just hidden in UI). Original columns preserved; extra fields are additive.
- `GET /api/talks/:id/rating` — own rating + aggregate for one talk (avg gated at >= 3).
- `POST /api/talks/:id/rating` — upsert own rating. UNIQUE(user_id, talk_id) makes a re-rating an UPDATE, never a duplicate. Validates talk exists + published and rating in 1..5 (400/404 otherwise). Returns fresh aggregate. No rewards/points path is ever touched.

### Frontend (user-portal/frontend/index.html)
- `TalkLibrary._cardHtml` gains an interactive star row: 5 tap targets with JS hover preview (`_hoverStars`/`_resetStars`), the member's own rating persisted as filled gold stars, a bilingual hint ("Rate this session" / "Vaša ocjena N od 5"), and the suppressed aggregate ("3.0 · 3 ratings"). Wrapper stops click propagation so rating never triggers the card's play handler.
- Optimistic `rate()` — reflects the tap immediately, reconciles with the server's authoritative aggregate, reverts on failure with a toast.
- New "Top rated" filter pill + `_renderTopRated` section: talks with count >= 3 sorted by avg desc then count desc then title; dignified empty state until three members have rated. Integrated with the existing year/event filters and `medx:localechange` relocalize.
- CSS for `.talk-rate`/`.talk-stars`/`.talk-rate-agg` (gold `--gold` fill, muted empty stars, hairline divider). i18n keys `talk.rateThis/yourRating/rateThanks/rateUpdated/rateError/topRated/topRatedSub/topRatedEmpty` (EN + HR formal Vi, correct diacritics). Croatian decimal comma + plural (`3,0 3 ocjene`).

### Quiet-profile
- Ratings ARE available to quiet members (feedback, not gamification) — the star UI is not quiet-gated. No points are awarded and no rewards surface is touched, so quiet profiles stay clean.

### Verify (E2E)
- API: three raters on one talk (5/4/3) -> count 3, avg 4.0; avg stayed null at count 1 and 2. Tester re-rated 5->2: DB kept exactly 3 rows / 3 distinct users (unique constraint held), avg recomputed to 3.0. Invalid rating -> 400, unknown talk -> 404.
- UI (playwright harness, desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844, EN + HR): 11 cards each with a star row, rated card shows filled stars + "3.0 · 3 ratings" (HR "3,0 · 3 ocjene"), hover preview fills to the hovered star, "Top rated" section shows only the qualifying talk. 0 console/page errors.
- Test rating rows deleted after verification (table persists empty across a restart). e2e.* accelerator rows untouched.

### Gates
- node --check user-portal/backend/server.js OK. scripts/check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (435 lines byte-identical — new table is outside the mirror block). No semicolons in user-facing copy, word "honest"/"honestly" absent. No git add/commit.

## 2026-07-06 — NO-API-KEY FALLBACK DEFECT FIXES (admin + shared/ai.js) — COMPLETE + VERIFIED
Scope = the defects from the 2026-07-06 No-API audit (MedX_NoAPI_Audit_2026-07-06/NO_API_AUDIT.md). Territory: admin-portal/ + shared/ai.js only. Edits via python3 unique-string replace with count==1 asserts (server.js + frontend index.html are giant); shared/ai.js via Edit. node --check clean on both JS files, schema-sync exit 0.

FIX 0 (root cause) — shared/ai.js: mockDraft() now returns EMPTY text and aiDraft() resolves to `{ text:'', mock:true, mock_reason }` on every no-key/failure path (no_key | rate_limited | http_error | timeout | empty). This revives the four Pattern-B `if(!text)` clean fallbacks that a truthy "[Draft] ..." dump used to shadow. mockDraft still exported (returns ''); _contextLines still used by the live path. User-portal home-feed caller already guards on `!draft.mock`, so it is unaffected (no mirror needed).

FIX 1/2/3 (Pattern-B email paths, now bilingual) — sponsorBuildFollowupEmail / sponsorBuildWrapEmail / nagBuildReminderEmail: each now uses `if (r && r.text && !r.mock && !/^\s*\[Draft\]/.test(r.text))` to accept a real model reply, else falls to a clean deterministic EN/HR fallback. Added top-level helper recipientPrefersCroatian({email,name,country,text}) — Croatian by stored country (users + registrations lookup by email), diacritics, -ic/-ovic surname, or Croatian words; defaults EN. HR subjects/bodies are em-dash-free; the 3 EN subjects I restructured had their pre-existing em-dashes normalized to colons.

FIX 4 (generic POST /api/admin/ai/draft) — added composeAdhocDraft(purpose,context,lang): titled paragraph + prose "For reference, x is y, ..." instead of the raw key dump. Model output gated on !mock. Optional lang='hr' mirrors to HR. Returns mock_reason.

FIX 5 (AI Inbox, highest auto-fire volume) — added aiInboxReplyFallback(msg,facts,{hr,firstName}): warm grounded reply keyed off the classified category + fine intents (thanks / cannot-attend / cost / dates) using live FAQ facts (dates, student/professional prices, venue, Gala = Hotel Esplanade Zagreb Dec 5). Bilingual by member language signal. Unclassifiable ('other' with no fine intent) stores NO draft (empty = the clean state, a human replies). aiInboxBuildDraft rewritten to use it on mock; auto-sweep stores clean or empty, never a dump.

FIX 6 (PR AI Studio stub, admin frontend) — generateImage() no longer alerts + saves an empty image record. It now builds a concrete on-brand design brief (buildImageBrief: campaign palette/mood + platform ratio/safe-zone) rendered as text, relabeled "Image Brief / Build image brief", saved as a useful `image_brief` history row, with Copy brief. generateCaption() -> buildCaption() is tone-aware + platform-aware (hashtag count/length) instead of a bare hashtag string.

ITEM 4 (venue) — Gala venue confirmed = Hotel Esplanade Zagreb. All 3 admin `Grand Ballroom, Zagreb` placeholders changed + a guarded value migration (never clobbers an owner-set venue). content_checklist "Plexus venue and partner hotel name" checked off via an idempotent, non-destructive migration (note-append is the one-time marker; never re-clobbers an owner reopen). Checklist now 9 open / 1 done. Cross-portal todo appended for the user-portal gala_settings default mirror + the empty-mock guard rule.

VERIFY (headless /tmp/medx-pw, admin :3002 real API + logged-in session, second libsql connection for seed/inspect):

- Fix 5: EN payment reply, HR dates+prices reply, HR thanks reply all clean/grounded; unclassifiable -> empty draft. 0 console errors.
- Fix 1/2/3: 6 then 5 staged outbox rows inspected — HAS_[Draft]=false on every one. Croatian recipients (Ivana Peric sponsor, Marko Horvat registrant country=Croatia) got HR subject+body; John Smith got EN. HR wrap benefits phrase localized ("cijeli paket partnerstva pred publikom od N").

## 2026-07-06 — AI-INBOX NO-KEY FALLBACK: two verified failures FIXED + E2E VERIFIED
Scope = ONLY the two reported failures in the offline (no ANTHROPIC key) AI-Inbox composer. Admin territory only (admin-portal/backend/server.js). Edits via python3 unique-string replace, every hunk asserted count==1. node --check OK, schema-sync exit 0. No git add/commit.

ROOT CAUSE (both) — aiInboxFaqFacts() pre-baked English-only fact strings (conference_dates = `${ISO} to ${ISO}`; student_price/professional_price = "student ticket EUR .. early bird / EUR .. late") that aiInboxReplyFallback() interpolated verbatim into the Croatian branch, and had no gala price fact at all while the gala line was gated behind `!dateLine` (always false).

FIX A (Failure 1 — HR leaked English) — aiInboxFaqFacts() now also returns a `raw` struct (confStart/confEnd, venue parts, the four conference prices, gala date/venue/prices) read live from plexus_settings + gala_settings. The composer builds the HR date/price lines from `raw` via two new localized helpers: aiInboxFmtDate() and aiInboxFmtDateRange() (HR months in the genitive — "prosinca"; compact same-month range "4. – 5. prosinca 2026."). HR prices are fully localized ("Kotizacije: za studente 39 EUR (rana prijava) / 59 EUR (kasna prijava), za profesionalce ..."). Country "Croatia" -> "Hrvatska" on the HR side. No ISO date, no English connector, no lowercase mid-sentence start. EN side unchanged in meaning, still clean.

FIX B (Failure 2 — gala answered with conference facts) — added an `asksGala` intent and a dedicated gala branch placed BEFORE the registration/cost branch. A gala question is now answered only with the gala's own date + venue + price (from gala_settings: 5 December 2026, Hotel Esplanade Zagreb, EUR 150 early bird / EUR 175 regular), never the conference EUR 39/59/99/149. Added a `gala_price` fact + venue to the model-facing facts text too, and removed the dead `!dateLine`-gated gala append (galaLine deleted, no dangling ref).

- Failure 2 GONE: HR gala draft = gala date+venue+price, no conference prices (39/59/99/149 absent), no conference range; EN gala draft = gala EUR 150/175, no conference prices. EN "ticket for the gala" classifies as registration question yet still hits the gala branch first -> correct gala facts.

## 2026-07-07 — EVENT REMINDERS: 4 adversarial-review failures FIXED + E2E PROVEN
Scope = admin-portal ONLY (backend server.js reminder engine + one admin panel copy line). No user-portal touch, no schema change. Edits via python3 unique-string replace (each hunk asserted count==1) on the giant admin server.js + index.html. node --check admin OK, schema-sync exit 0 (435 lines byte-identical — nothing mirrored). Verified against an ISOLATED VACUUM-INTO snapshot of shared/medx_portal.db on a throwaway admin instance (DATABASE_PATH override, PORT 3092, nohup, /tmp log); the real shared DB was READ-ONLY and confirmed untouched (0 reminder_sequences, 0 event-reminder rows after). No git add/commit.

FAILURE 1 [major] — conference reminder audience silently EXCLUDED real member-portal registrants. reminderAudience('conference') read registrations.email, which is NULL for the frozen POST /api/registrations flow (user-portal server.js:9573 inserts user_id and no email/first/last/country). FIX (no frozen code touched): the conference SELECT now `FROM registrations r LEFT JOIN users u ON r.user_id = u.id` with COALESCE(r.email,u.email) / first_name / last_name / country, and every WHERE email predicate re-expressed over COALESCE(r.email,u.email). Mirrors the pre-existing postEventCheckedIn survey join, now applied pre-event. PROVEN: on the isolated snapshot the two genuinely-confirmed member-flow rows (confirmed|free = "Tess Away" via throwaway.plexus+…@example.com, confirmed|paid = "Ivan Horvat" via ivan.horvat@test.medx.hr) both carry NULL registrations.email yet now appear in the audience — /audience count = 4 and a T-30 run staged 4 (was 2: only the boris/carla comp rows that happened to carry literal @kbc.hr emails). Admin card now reads "4 registered" (was 2). Ivan resolves to HR (users.country Croatia), Tess to EN.

FAILURE 2 [minor] — approved/confirmed-but-UNPAID registrants received a reminder carrying the non-refundable footer. DECISION (explicit, per the builder's ask): the non-refundable small-print now appears ONLY for a registrant who has actually PAID money — a held (approved/confirmed but unpaid), comp, free, or free-event seat has nothing that is non-refundable, so it warmly still gets the reminder but WITHOUT that footer. Reach is unchanged (audience still includes held seats). Implemented by carrying a `paid` flag on each audience person (conference/gala paid = payment_status==='paid'; bridges/donor free events = false) and gating remFineprint on person.paid in remBuildTouch; the preview person is treated as paid so the admin template preview still shows the full footer. Admin panel description corrected from "with the non-refundable note in the footer" to "…shown to guests who have paid." PROVEN (gala T-7, as_of 2026-11-28, audience 9 = 6 paid + 3 approved-unpaid): all 6 paid staged emails carry the footer in the right language (5 EN "non-refundable", maria.kovac Croatian "ne podliježu povratu sredstava"), all 3 approved-unpaid (ivana.maric/tomislav.peric/hans.mueller) carry NO footer; the all-comp/free conference batch carries 0 footers.

FAILURE 3 [minor] — the pause kill switch stopped future staging but did NOT recall rows already staged as status='scheduled', so a batch staged seconds before the pause still drained out. FIX (generalizes the accelerator revert precedent at server.js ~12819): POST …/pause now, in addition to setting status='paused', cancels this sequence's still-scheduled reminder emails (UPDATE scheduled_emails SET status='cancelled' WHERE source_engine='event-reminder' AND status='scheduled' AND batch_id LIKE 'eventreminder-<seqId8>-%'), clears their per-recipient-per-touch drip marks (reminder:<eventKey>:<touch>:<email>, touch parsed from the batch_id) and the per-day tick markers, so the recall is a clean reversible undo; returns `recalled` and logs the count. PROVEN: after a gala run staged 9 'scheduled' rows, an immediate pause flipped all 9 → 'cancelled' (drainer now skips them), drip 9→0 and day-tick→0, pause returned recalled:9; a forced run while paused = not_approved (new staging still blocked); resume + re-run cleanly RE-STAGED 9 fresh 'scheduled' rows (9 scheduled + 9 cancelled audit trail) — no data loss, no double-send.

FAILURE 4 [minor] — batch_id embedded the wall-clock date instead of the simulated as_of/logical send date. FIX: runReminderTick builds batchId from `asOf` (the tick's logical date) not the real-clock `today`; the now-unused `today` local was removed. Drip dedup keys were already date-independent so no send behavior changes — purely makes the outbox batch_id truthful. PROVEN: a conference run with as_of=2026-11-04 produced batch_id eventreminder-…-t30-2026-11-04-… and a gala run with as_of=2026-11-28 produced …-t7-2026-11-28-… (both the logical date, not 2026-07-07).

VERIFY SUMMARY: 59 changed lines in admin server.js (diff vs pre-edit backup = exactly the four fixes + the preview-paid tweak + the pause response, no collateral) plus 1 admin copy line in index.html. Admin reminders panel re-rendered through the real logged-in UI (headless /tmp/medx-pw, desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844): both sequence cards render, Plexus Conference shows "4 registered" and Gala "9 registered", ZERO console errors / ZERO pageerrors on both viewports. node --check admin OK, schema-sync exit 0. Copy: no semicolons in member-facing strings, banned word "honest" absent, admin surface EN, member footer bilingual (formal Vi, diacritics). Frozen surfaces (Stripe/checkout/calculateTotal/registrations POST/QR gen+scanner/existing links/accelerator) untouched. Isolated snapshot + scratch scripts removed; harness e2e-reminders-ui.js kept. No git add/commit.

## 2026-07-07 — A-autoreply FIX: auto-reply language now follows the INBOUND MESSAGE, not the invitee's country
Scope = admin-portal/backend/server.js AUTO-REPLY ENGINE ONLY (arDetectLang/arClassify/arComposeReply/arMaybeAutoReply). Invitation + reminder engines and every frozen surface (Stripe/calculateTotal/POST-registrations/QR+scanner/member-QR/payment-invite links/legacy accelerator) untouched. Edits via python3 unique-string replace, each hunk asserted count==1 (7 hunks). node --check admin OK, check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (435 lines byte-identical, nothing mirrored — no schema change; auto_reply_log.lang column already existed). No git add/commit.

THE BUG (verified major): arComposeReply() and the gate arMaybeAutoReply() picked the reply language via eiIsCroatian(inv), which reads ONLY inv.country. The deterministic classifier arClassify() detected HR vs EN from the message but discarded it (returned only {category,confidence}). So an imported invitee with a blank/foreign country writing in Croatian — or a Croatia-country diaspora writing in English — got the auto-reply in the WRONG language, and auto_reply_log recorded the wrong lang. This is member-facing and breaks the EN+HR "bilingual correct per inbound language" standard.

THE FIX (minimal, root cause): new arDetectLang(text) — deterministic, auditable, no key: Croatian diacritics (cez cus etc., +2) and HR function words weighed against EN function words; returns 'hr', 'en', or null when too short/ambiguous. arClassify() now also returns lang = arDetectLang(text) (additive — {category,confidence,lang}). The gate derives hr = cls.lang ? (cls.lang==='hr') : eiIsCroatian(inv) and passes cls.lang into arComposeReply(inv,camp,facts,category,tpl,lang), whose hr now = (lang==='hr')?true:(lang==='en')?false:eiIsCroatian(inv). So the DETECTED message language wins; the stored country is only a fallback when the text is indeterminate. The audit log's lang (derived from the same hr) is now correct too. The /reply-templates/:category/preview call was left unchanged: it sets sample.country to match its own hr toggle, so the country fallback still renders the right preview.

E2E PROOF (admin :3002, shared dev DB; restarted to load the change): created a gala campaign, imported 3 invitees, approved all 3 templates, armed the campaign, injected 3 mock inbounds:
- CASE A (the exact repro) — Dr. Marco Rossi country='United Kingdom' writes Croatian "Koja je cijena i gdje se odrzava?" -> auto_sent question; composed email is CROATIAN: subject "Pojedinosti koje ste zatrazili", greeting "Postovani/postovana Dr. Marco Rossi,", HR markers present, ZERO EN markers; auto_reply_log lang='hr'. (Before the fix this went out English "Dear Dr. Marco Rossi / The details you asked for".)
- CASE B (the reverse) — Dr. Ivan Horvat country='Croatia' writes English "What is the price and where is it held?" -> auto_sent question; composed email is ENGLISH: subject "The details you asked for", greeting "Dear Dr. Ivan Horvat,", EN markers present, ZERO HR markers; lang='en'.
- CASE C (control) — Dr. Ana Kovac country='Croatia' writes Croatian -> HR, unchanged/correct; lang='hr'.
No server errors in the log; both dev servers healthy (:3001/:3002). Copy: no semicolons in member-facing EN/HR strings, formal Vi, banned word "honest" absent. Cleaned ALL test junk — deleted the test campaign (cascade replies+invitees), unapproved the 3 templates back to their default DRAFT state, purged auto_reply_settings/auto_reply_log/auto-reply scheduled_emails/drip_log rows for this campaign; residue check = 0 across the board. e2e.* + accelerator worked-example rows preserved.

## 2026-07-07 — A-autoreply FIX 2: arDetectLang ' i ' false-positive on English 'I' + margin guard
Scope = admin-portal/backend/server.js AUTO-REPLY ENGINE ONLY (arDetectLang + the /reply-templates/:category/preview compose call). Every other surface untouched. Edits via python3 unique-string replace, each hunk asserted count==1 (3 hunks). node --check admin OK, check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (435 lines byte-identical — no schema change). No git.

THE BUG (verified major, deterministic): arDetectLang() carried ' i ' in the Croatian function-word list (to catch Croatian 'and'), but after the text is lowercased ' i ' also matches the English pronoun 'I'. Short, unambiguous English replies scored Croatian: "I accept." / "I'm afraid I can't make it." / "I have to decline, sorry." each hit ' i ' (hr=1, en=0) -> arDetectLang returned 'hr'. Because the gate derives hr = cls.lang ? (cls.lang==='hr') : eiIsCroatian(inv), a non-null buggy 'hr' OVERRODE the country fallback, so a UK invitee's plain-English acceptance was answered with a FULLY CROATIAN email and auto_reply_log recorded lang='hr' — a regression of the just-shipped "reply mirrors the inbound language" standard.

THE FIX (root cause, two parts): (1) removed ' i ' from the Croatian token list — it is unsafe against English 'I'; Croatian detection stays strong via its other function words and the diacritic vote (č ć ž š đ already weighted +2, which alone clears the guard). (2) MARGIN GUARD on the decision: arDetectLang now returns a language ONLY when the signal is DECISIVE — hr-en >= 2 -> 'hr', en-hr >= 2 -> 'en', otherwise null so the existing eiIsCroatian(country) fallback decides. A lone Croatian diacritic yields hr=2/en=0 (margin 2) and is therefore decisive on its own, exactly as specified; weak/ambiguous text no longer overrides the country. (3) minor: the /reply-templates/:category/preview route now passes its chosen language through — arComposeReply(sample, {cta_url}, facts, cat, draftTpl, hr ? 'hr' : 'en') — so the preview's HR/EN toggle is authoritative instead of relying on the sample's country matching.

VERIFY — unit matrix (arDetectLang + arClassify extracted VERBATIM into a standalone node test, all PASS): "I accept." -> lang=null (never hr), cat=coming; "I'm afraid I can't make it." -> null (never hr), cat=declining; "I have to decline, sorry." -> null (never hr), cat=declining; "Dolazim, hvala na pozivu!" -> hr, coming; "Nažalost ne mogu doći." -> hr (diacritics), declining; "Potvrđujem dolazak" -> hr (diacritic), coming; ambiguous "ok" -> null (country fallback), complex.

VERIFY — live path on an ISOLATED snapshot (VACUUM INTO /tmp/medx_ar_snapshot.db; second instance PORT=3093, NODE_ENV=development, blank email/Turso keys, dev-auth admin; seeded a gala campaign + armed auto_reply_settings + approved 'coming' template + a UK invitee jane.whitfield@example.co.uk country='United Kingdom'): POST /replies/mock-inbound body="I accept." -> auto_sent coming 0.90; auto_reply_log.lang='en'; staged scheduled_emails row status='scheduled', subject "We are delighted you will join us", body "Dear Dr. Jane Whitfield, Thank you for confirming..." — ZERO Croatian markers. (Before this fix the same input produced a fully Croatian email + lang='hr'.) 3093 killed by port, snapshot + temp test files removed. Canonical :3002 restarted by port on the fixed code (HTTP 200, healthy).

## 2026-07-07 — ADMIN ASSISTANT deterministic core upgrade (queue 5a6, NO-KEY half) BUILT + E2E VERIFIED

Upgraded the robot-head Med&X Assistant (admin-portal ONLY) so the NO-KEY deterministic/retrieval path is the GOOD baseline: grounded how-to, can-I-build, and live-data answers with working deep links. The agentic do-it-for-me mode stays key-gated behind a clean gate card. No schema changes (KB is static in code, all data reads are read-only) — check-schema-sync.sh exit 0.

### Backend (admin-portal/backend/server.js)
- New grounded-retrieval core inserted before ASSIST_PRECISE_KINDS: ASSIST_KB (19 articles = 12 Operations Handbook sections + 4 Runbook sections + 3 live-feature entries), each with title, where, plain-text steps, a watch-out, keyword set, and a DEEP LINK target. Corpus mirrors the handbook/runbook HTML in admin resources (incl. "Grow the Biomedical Forum" + "Run the Med&X Accelerator").
- assistTok() tokenizer + ASSIST_STOP; kwset/titleTok precomputed per article.
- assistKbMatch(lc): fires only on how-to ("how do/where do/how to...") or can-build ("can I/is it possible/does the portal...") phrasing; scores the corpus (kw hit=2, title token=1), threshold >=3 or returns null (never guesses). Yes+steps+deep link for can-build; how-to lead for how-to.
- assistNotSupported(lc): can-build NO answers with the REAL alternative — SMS/WhatsApp -> Direct notification; post-to-social -> Publer/PR & Media; auto-charge -> Stripe/Finances reconcile; bulk wipe -> Runbook restore; edit marketing site -> Portal Content.
- assistDataMatch(lc): grounded live numbers from the real DB (read-only): gala seats-left (capacity - confirmed), gala guests, forum campaign status+funnel, bridges regs, checked-in, unpaid, revenue, members, contacts, Plexus registrations. Excludes how-to phrasing; requires a quantity word; returns null when no confident metric (no invented facts).
- assistDeterministicAnswer wired: precise draft/contact (unchanged) -> assistDataMatch -> assistKbMatch -> null. Loose "any how many -> registrations" fallback REMOVED (was a fact-invention risk).
- ASSIST_PRECISE_KINDS += howto, canbuild, data (grounded, run in BOTH modes). Endpoint returns deepLink; no-key + unmatched: action-shaped -> clean {gated:true} gate card, else graceful handbook hand-off (never a guess). With a key, write-actions still fall through to the live agent (assistRunAgent untouched).

### Frontend (admin-portal/frontend/index.html)
- Kept the robot-head FAB + entry points. Empty state now shows 4 example chips (assistAsk) + subtitle/placeholder refreshed. assistantSend captures res.deepLink + res.gated onto history; assistantRender draws an "Open X" .assist-deeplink button under answers and a lock-headed .assist-gatecard for gated replies.
- App.assistDeepLink(target): resolver mapping 24 targets to the SAME nav the Cmd+K palette uses (App.showSection / showProject / showForumTab / FinanceApp.showTab('reconcile') / openGlobalQRScanner / NetworkApp.openPanel), closes the assistant then navigates. ## 2026-07-07 — U-faqbot FIXES: medical over-match, invented venue facts, off-topic confidence, HR typo miss (4 verified failures)

Scope = user-portal/backend/faq-kb.js + ONE hunk in user-portal/backend/server.js (the /api/assistant/ask strong-match gate, python3 unique-string replace asserted count==1). Nothing else touched. node --check clean on both, check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (435 lines byte-identical — no schema change). FROZEN untouched (Stripe/checkout, calculateTotal, POST /api/registrations, QR gen+scanner+member QR payload, payment/invite links, legacy accelerator). No git.

FIX 1 (major) — medical detector rebuilt for a MEDICAL audience. The old MED_RE was an unanchored substring alternation, so 'crashed' tripped 'rash' and ordinary program vocabulary (cancer, depression, infection, antibiotic, pregnancy, treatment) refused legitimate member questions. Replaced with a tiered, WORD-BOUNDED detector: (1) MED_ADVICE_RE — explicit personal clinical-advice asks (should I take, is it safe to take, dosage, N mg, side effects, chest pain, my rash, trebam li uzeti, koju dozu, boli me, imam osip...) ALWAYS decline, even when an event is mentioned; (2) health TOPIC words alone are NOT enough — they decline only when personally framed (I have, my, am I, do I need...) AND free of program/event/portal context (session, talk, speaker, research, venue, room, certificate, regist*, portal, site, gala, sesij*, predavanj*, konferencij*...). All nine reported false positives now route normally, all genuine clinical asks still decline (EN + HR).

FIX 2 (major) — removed the three INVENTED venue facts from buildFacts (venue_address street number, nearest_transit two-minute-walk, parking_info valet/garages — none of which exist in any DB/config source, and the vetted member_faq_kb.md leaves them as undefined placeholders). The module's existing rule-11 mechanism now does exactly what the contract promises: L2/L3 render with an unresolved placeholder -> ok=false -> warm hand-off to the team. The campaign's named bait 'is parking free at the Esplanade?' now ESCALATES (was: confident L3 answer asserting unsourced valet-parking claims). venue_name+venue_city stay grounded from the conferences row, gala venue from gala_settings, so QCONF/QGALA still answer with the real venue. Comment left at the removal site: resolve these facts from a real config source when one exists, never author them in code.

FIX 3 (minor) — off-topic questions now hand off instead of answering the wrong entry. scoreAll returns two new signals per result: hits (query tokens this entry matched, exact or fuzzy) and oov (informative tokens length>=3, non-numeric, that NO entry in the whole KB recognises — 'dog', 'wifi'). The ask gate requires covered = hits>=2 OR oov===0 before a confident answer. 'can I bring my dog to the conference' (1 hit + OOV dog) and 'what is the wifi password' (1 hit + OOV wifi) both escalate gracefully now. Chatty-but-on-topic stays answered ('hi when is the gala this year' -> QGALA) because greetings joined STOP (hi/hello/hey/thanks/thank/dear + bok/pozdrav/hvala/postovani/postovana, plus HR function words ima/jesu) and 'year' is KB-known. Bonus: T7 keywords gained crash/crashed/error/frozen + srusio/pao/greska/zamrznuo so the portal-crash report (freed from the medical trap by FIX 1) lands a real T7 tech answer instead of a generic hand-off.

FIX 4 (minor) — one-edit typo tolerance in the fuzzy matcher. New oneEditApart() (single substitution, insertion, deletion, or ADJACENT TRANSPOSITION, tokens >=6 chars, scored 1.5 like the stem match) catches what the common-prefix check misses: 'regsitrirati' ~ 'registrirati' shares only a 3-char prefix but is one transposition away. 'kako se regsitrirati za konferencju' now answers R1 (how to register, norm 2.12) instead of QCONF, with margin 0.61 over QCONF.

VERIFY — module harness (real faq-kb.js + shared/medx_portal.db via libsql, gate logic mirrored VERBATIM from server.js): 49/49 PASS — 9 ex-false-positives in scope, 12 genuine medical still decline (EN+HR), 3 venue-logistics escalations incl. the parking bait, dog+wifi escalate, HR typo -> R1, 21-case flagship regression battery green (QCONF/QGALA/QPRICE/QAPPLY EN+HR, R1/R7/P1/P3/C1/M1/M6/L4/L6/X2 routes, gibberish escalates), and buildFacts asserted to expose NO parking_info/venue_address/nearest_transit while plexus_dates/plexus_venue/gala_date stay live from the DB.

VERIFY — E2E live: user :3001 restarted on the fixed code (kill by PORT, explicit PORT=3001, nohup, /tmp/medx-user-3001.log, NODE_ENV=development, shared dev DB). 18-question curl matrix through POST /api/assistant/ask matches the harness exactly (crash->T7 answer, cancer-session/breast-cancer/antibiotic/infection-control->escalate, medication-before-gala + headache + HR boli-me->medical decline, parking bait + address->escalate, dog + wifi->escalate, HR typo->R1 answer, QCONF 4-5 December 2026 Esplanade / QPRICE 150-200 EUR / QGALA 5 December / HR QCONF prosinca / P3 refund holding line all unchanged). Browser (headless, real member JWT for tester@medx.local): desktop 1440x900 AND mobile 390x844 through the actual chat panel — crash question gets the T7 answer with helpful-vote chips, wifi gets the hand-off with the 'Povežite me s timom' button, HR typo answers R1, kada-je-konferencija answers QCONF with live dates, medication declines; ZERO console errors on both viewports (the PWA install banner needed dismissing on mobile to reach the FAB — pre-existing layout, not a defect). ## 2026-07-07 — 5a5b LIVE NOW/NEXT EVENT BOARD (user-portal only)

Built the premium during-event "happening now / up next" board for Plexus (and reusable per event). Display-only surface over the EXISTING single source of truth — reads ConferenceData.schedule, the same array the program tab renders via ConferenceData.getScheduleByDay(day) and My Schedule uses. NO schedule fork, NO new tables, NO server change (check-schema-sync.sh exit 0, 435 lines byte-identical), FROZEN paths untouched (Stripe/checkout, calculateTotal, POST /api/registrations, QR gen+scanner+member QR payload, payment/invite links, legacy accelerator).

WHAT: self-contained module (window.PlexusLiveBoard) + scoped CSS (#pxlbStyles) + one mount div at the top of the Schedule panel (.pxlb-mount / #pxLiveBoardMount). Injected into the 6.4MB frontend/index.html via python3 unique-string splices (asserted count==1 each): CSS before #medxI18nStyles, mount after #px-panel-schedule open, module before the document body close.

STATE MACHINE: each session gets absolute start/end (dayDates {1:2026-12-04,2:2026-12-05} + time/endTime; +60min default when endTime missing) compared to the clock -> done / now / upcoming. Focused day = day of the current-or-next session. NOW = every live session (parallel rooms shown side by side), UP NEXT = the next-to-start session(s) incl. parallel starts, LATER TODAY = remaining upcoming (dimmed) with an "N completed" count, past dimmed/collapsed. Client tick every 30s recomputes and AUTO-ADVANCES with no reload; a real-time-advancing mock clock (?now=ISO) drives testing/demo.

GATING: the board surfaces only when now is inside the event window [firstStart-60min, lastEnd+30min]; otherwise it is display:none and the Schedule builder renders as today. Admin/testing preview via ?livepreview=1. A subtle crimson pulse marks the Schedule tab when live.

BIG-SCREEN: ?display=1 mounts a chrome-free full-screen projector overlay (z-index max, near-black navy + gold, huge Fraunces titles, CET clock, LIVE pill, auto-cycling NOW pages every 7s, UP NEXT strip). Works with no login (projector-friendly). ConferenceData.init() seeds defaults if the store is cold.

BILINGUAL: all board copy via MedXI18n.extend (plb.* keys, EN + HR formal Vi, no semicolons) + medx:localechange re-render; dates via MedXI18n.formatDate (hr-HR/en-US). Session titles/room names stay as data (never machine-translated). Navy/gold brand, mobile excellent (parallel NOW cards stack).

- In-page Schedule tab (logged in tester@medx.local): 11:45 state = NOW Neural Circuits (Room A) + UP NEXT Lunch + LATER x3 + "4 completed" + past dimmed; 14:30 = two-room parallel NOW; EN and HR both render; desktop 1440x900 AND mobile 390x844 (parallel cards stack); gating OFF with the real clock hides the board (display:none) and the builder shows. 0 console errors.

FIX 1 (major) — QPRICE subject gate. Generic money tokens ('much','cost','koliko','kosta') live in QPRICE's keywords so it surfaces on a real ticket-price question, but those words alone satisfied the covered guard (hits>=2 or oov==0) and the +0.45 prio nudge lifted QPRICE over threshold, so off-topic cost questions got quoted ticket prices ('how much does a taxi from the airport cost', 'how much does dinner cost in Zagreb', 'how much is parking at the venue', 'how much does it cost to submit an abstract', 'Koliko kosta taksi od zracne luke'). Added QPRICE_SUBJECT_RE + priceHasTicketSubject(text) in faq-kb.js (word-bounded, deburr-folded, EN+HR): a ticket/registration SUBJECT must be named (ticket, registration, register, admission, entry, pass, seat, delegate, attendance, conference/registration/entry fee, day pass; ulaznic*, kotizacij*, karta/karte/kartu, registracij*, prijav*, sjedal*). server.js covered gate now ANDs priceSubjectOk = top is not QPRICE OR priceHasTicketSubject(question); a QPRICE hit without a ticket-subject fails covered -> warm hand-off. Implemented on QPRICE specifically (no global threshold change), so the 15 passing checks are untouched. All 5 off-topic cost asks (+ hotel/flight variants) escalate; 'how much is a general ticket?' and 'koliko kosta kotizacija?' still answer QPRICE with 150/200 prices.

FIX 2 (major) — assistant FAB no longer covers the QR check-in FAB. The gold assistant bubble (.mxa-fab) sat at right:24px bottom:88px z-index:9999 — byte-identical position/size/z-index to the pre-existing #floatingQRBtn (desktop), so it exactly covered one-click check-in QR. Moved the desktop .mxa-fab to bottom:152px, stacking it cleanly ABOVE the QR fab (88px), which sits above the '?' help fab (24px). Mobile (<=640px) slot unchanged (bottom:140px + safe-area; QR fab is display:none on mobile by design). Verified at 1440x900: three distinct non-overlapping rects (assistant 692-748, QR 756-812, help 828-876) and document.elementFromPoint at each center returns the element itself (owns=true for all three); mobile 390x844: assistant visible + clickable + within viewport, QR hidden.

FIX 3 (minor) — tier-1 medical detector gained the program-context exemption tier-2 already had. MED_ADVICE_RE tier-1 ('side effects?', 'what (medication|medicine|dose|dosage)', 'dose of') was declining genuine program questions ('will the immunotherapy session cover side effects?', 'what medicine topics are on the program?', 'the talk mentioned a dose of radiation, will slides be shared?'). Added MED_TALK_CONTEXT_RE — a NARROWER curriculum-context set (session/talk/lecture/topic/agenda/program/schedule/slides/presentation/panel/workshop/keynote/abstract/poster/speaker/curriculum + HR sesij*/predavanj*/tem[ae]*/raspored*/radionic*/prezentacij*/izlaganj*/slajd*), deliberately excluding gala/ticket/venue so a real advice ask that only names an event as timing still declines. isMedicalQuestion tier-1 now returns !MED_TALK_CONTEXT_RE.test(t). The three program-context asks route to normal retrieval/escalation (not medical); 'what medication should I take before the gala' and 'What dose of ibuprofen should I take for my headache?' still decline.

## 2026-07-07 — 5a5f LIVE AUCTION ENGINE (admin-portal, event-agnostic, public bid + display served from admin)

Built the reusable charity-auction capability. EVENT-AGNOSTIC by construction (owner hard requirement): tables/routes/UI all neutral "auction" (auctions / auction_items / auction_bids ; /api/admin/auctions ; /a/:token ; /api/public/auction/:token), attachable to ANY event via event_key + event_name, themed per event (theme_accent). Gala is only the first user — ZERO gala_ in any new identifier (grep-proven; the sole "gala_" hit is a comment saying "never gala_*"). Admin-only; user-portal server.js UNTOUCHED. Public bid + big-screen display are served from the ADMIN backend like the /e/:token combo pages.

SCHEMA (admin server.js, OUTSIDE SCHEMA-MIRROR:END — admin-only, shared Turso gets them on admin boot; check-schema-sync.sh exit 0, 435 lines byte-identical): auctions (token, event_key, event_name, theme_accent, title, subtitle, status[draft/live/paused/closed], bank_details, donation_note, active_item_id), auction_items (starting_bid, min_increment, currency, status[pending/open/paused/closed/sold], winning_bid_id, winner_name/email, final_amount, payment_status[none/pledged/paid], confirmed_at, paid_at, reminder_count), auction_bids (bidder_name, paddle, amount, source[public/staff], status[active/void], ip) + 2 indexes.

BACKEND: full admin CRUD + lifecycle (create/edit/delete auction; item CRUD; open/pause/close a lot -> opening sets the active on-screen lot, flips the auction live, and pauses any other open lot so one lot is live at a time; auction-level pause/resume/close). Bids flow through ONE server-validated stream shared by the zero-login public page AND staff paddle entry: amount must clear the floor (highest active bid + min_increment, else starting_bid); name-or-paddle required; auction must be live and the active lot open. Read-validate-insert is synchronous (no await between), so racing bids order correctly and the loser is rejected against the just-raised floor. Void recomputes the highest. Confirm-winner marks the lot sold + pledged and stages a warm BILINGUAL DONATION email (EN + HR formal Vi, binding-pledge line, amount, purpose, bank-transfer block) in the EXISTING approval outbox (scheduled_emails pending_approval, source_engine='auction-winner') — bank details come from a per-auction settings field, NEVER a hardcoded IBAN; empty -> placeholder block in the email + bank_missing warning to the UI. Mark-paid tracks pledged->paid; unpaid reminder (source_engine='auction-reminder') is idempotent per round via drip_log marker auction-reminder:<item>:<n>. auction-summary?event_key= feeds the command-center numbers (lots/sold/pledged/paid/outstanding). Public bid endpoint rate-limited (20/min/IP). New QR endpoint returns data-URLs for the two public URLs (plain URL strings — the frozen member-QR generation/validation is untouched). FROZEN surfaces (Stripe/checkout, calculateTotal, POST /api/registrations, QR gen+scanner+member QR payload, payment/invite links, legacy accelerator) all untouched — additive only.

PUBLIC PAGES (admin-served, zero-login, EN+HR toggle, no semicolons in user copy, formal Vi): /a/:token = elegant phone bid page for senior guests (event + lot + photo, current bid, min-next, name + table/paddle + amount with quick-raise chips, server-validated, polls state every 3s, rate-limit-aware). /a/:token?display=1 = premium navy/gold big-screen projector (huge current bid in gold, leader, min-next, recent-bid ticker, LIVE/UŽIVO pill, polls every 2.5s, AUTO-ADVANCES when the admin switches the active lot). Client JS uses addEventListener + string concatenation (no backtick / no ${} so the server template literal is safe; grep-verified).

FRONTEND (admin index.html, additive splices asserted count==1): nav item "Auctions" after Gala, section-auctions, sectionNames entry, showSection init hook, AuctionAdmin module (list+create, detail with status controls + settings + QR modal + live-polling monitor, add-lot with photo via the EXISTING /api/upload/photos boundary, staff paddle-bid modal, void, confirm-winner modal with bank-missing warning, mark-paid, reminder), PendingOutbox.LABELS gains "Auction winner" / "Auction reminder". Admin UI is English (per house rule). Inline onclick handlers fire under the admin CSP (script-src-attr 'unsafe-inline' set).

VERIFY (E2E, admin :3002 restarted on final code — kill by PORT, explicit PORT=3002, nohup, /tmp log, NODE_ENV=development, shared dev DB, NO ANTHROPIC key; minted admin JWT for the browser):

Void -> highest recomputed (300->250). Staff paddle 250 OK + staff below-increment REJECT. SCHEMA (admin server.js, OUTSIDE the SCHEMA-MIRROR block — admin-only, the user portal never reads it): event_editions (id, project, edition_key UNIQUE e.g. plexus-2026, label, status active|archived, start_date, end_date, venue, conference_id, talk_library_eligible, moments_tag, config_snapshot_json, carried_from, is_seed, archived_at, created_by, created/updated_at) + 2 indexes. Placed right after the auction indexes; node --check clean, schema-sync green.

MIGRATION: ensureEditionsSeeded() (lazy, idempotent, called at the top of every editions route) registers the CURRENT live data as each project's ACTIVE edition (plexus from the active conference, bridges/forum/accelerator/gala from project_settings) WITHOUT touching any live table. Only saveDb() when it actually inserts. Seeded 5 real editions on first hit (plexus-2026 linked to the real Esplanade conference, etc.) — this is the desired data migration, not junk.

ARCHIVE (state change, NEVER a delete): POST /editions/:id/archive-preview = dry run listing exactly what changes (status active->archived) and everything preserved (registrations, tickets valid, certificates, sessions) + routed to history (Talk Library eligibility flag, Moments photo tag) with destructive:false. POST /editions/:id/archive (confirm:true required) snapshots the then-current config into config_snapshot_json (page_settings + ticket_types + sessions + speakers + sponsors verbatim), flips status, sets talk_library_eligible=1 + moments_tag=edition_key. Touches NO registration/ticket/certificate/conference.is_active — so every issued QR keeps validating byte-identically through the untouched scanner.

CARRY-OVER WIZARD (create the next edition): POST /editions/:id/carryover-preview returns defaults (year+1, dates shifted, label re-yeared) + a live preview of what carries (pricing structure / program skeleton / sponsor tiers, each with its source count) vs what resets (speakers->0, registrations->0, dates/countdown->new). POST /editions/:id/carryover (confirm:true) creates a NEW conferences row (is_active=1, year N+1 — the member portal picks it up automatically), clones only the chosen dimensions (ticket_types with sold_count reset, sessions with speaker bindings cleared, sponsors), updates plexus_page_settings.conference_date ONLY for project==plexus (curated copy left intact), refreshes project_settings dates, and inserts the new ACTIVE event_editions row (carried_from = source). Old edition + its registrations/tickets/certs stay exactly as they were.

FRONTEND (admin index.html, additive splices asserted count==1): nav item "Editions" after Year Calendar, sectionNames entry, section-editions (scoped CSS), showSection init hook -> EditionsAdmin.init(), and the EditionsAdmin module. Per-project cards (active solid / archived dashed + In-Talk-Library / In-Moments / N-preserved chips + View-snapshot), an archive dry-run modal, a conversational carry-over wizard (name/dates/venue + carry toggles with on-file counts + live resets/preview populated on open) and a snapshot viewer. Admin copy is English; inline onclick under the admin CSP. Globals are top-level lexical consts (accessed bare by inline handlers, NOT on window) — same pattern as AuctionAdmin.

## 2026-07-07 — 5a5d FOLLOW-UP FIX: carryover forward-path member portal now follows the new edition

Fixed the one verified defect in Event Edition Cycling: the carryover FORWARD path claimed "the member portal shows the active edition with ZERO user-portal changes," but that held only for /api/conferences/active, /api/public/site and getActiveConference() (pricing/registration). Every member-FACING route was hardcoded to slug='plexus-2026', so after a one-click "Start next edition" a member who registered for the carried-over edition could not retrieve their certificate, visa letter, hotels or survey, and the schedule kept serving the old year. Repro confirmed live (admin carryover -> plexus-2099 active; a member registered + checked-in under 2099): my-certificate 400, visa-request 400, hotels [], survey null, schedule.conference.slug=plexus-2026. NOTE: the ticket QR wallet was already fine — /api/registrations/my returns all of a member's registrations, never slug-filtered — and the archive half was already flawless.

ROOT CAUSE + SCOPE: 5 member routes in user-portal/backend/server.js resolved the conference by literal slug. Fixed exactly those 5 (+1 helper); nothing else. FROZEN surfaces untouched and grep-verified after the edit: the member QR payloads (conf:'plexus-2026' at reg-QR gen, lines 9621 + 17951), the seed row (line 7999), POST /api/registrations, and getActiveConference()'s pricing fallback all still carry their plexus-2026 literals byte-for-byte. Admin server + user frontend UNTOUCHED. No schema change -> check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (435 lines byte-identical).

THE FIX (7 python3 unique-string edits, each asserted count==1; node --check clean; plexus-2026 literal count 59 -> 54):
- New helper activePlexusConf() right after getActiveConference(): returns conferences WHERE is_active=1 ORDER BY year DESC LIMIT 1, falling back to the seed row — the SAME contract the portal header (/api/conferences/active) and the public site already trust, so the whole member portal follows a carried-over edition. Content routes now call it: /api/plexus/schedule, /api/plexus/hotels, /api/plexus/survey, /api/plexus/sessions (each keeps a null-guard).
- Personal routes resolve the MEMBER's own registration instead of a fixed slug, preferring the active edition then their newest: /api/plexus/my-certificate and /api/plexus/visa-request now filter WHERE r.user_id=? [AND r.checked_in=1] ORDER BY c.is_active DESC, c.year DESC LIMIT 1. This is what preserves the FROZEN "valid FOREVER" invariant: a past attendee whose edition was archived still resolves their OWN older registration and re-downloads their certificate/letter — nothing is deleted, the route simply stops pinning one slug. my-certificate additionally now stamps the reg's actual conference name + a PLX<yy>-CERT number (a 2099 attendee no longer gets a "Plexus Conference 2026" certificate).

VERIFY (E2E on both portals restarted on the fixed code — kill by PORT, explicit PORT=3001/3002, nohup, /tmp logs, shared dev DB shared/medx_portal.db; admin carryover via the real /api/admin/editions/:id/carryover, member registered via the frozen POST /api/registrations, checked-in, minted member JWT on the user JWT_SECRET):
- BEFORE (repro, broken): my-certificate 400 "Must attend", visa-request 400 "Must be registered", hotels count 0, survey absent, schedule.slug=plexus-2026.
- AFTER (fixed): my-certificate 200 name="Plexus Conference 2099" number="PLX99-CERT-...", visa-request 200 success, hotels=["E2E Hotel 2099"], survey title="E2E Survey 2099", schedule.slug=plexus-2099 with the 2099 session; ticket wallet /api/registrations/my still shows the 2099 ticket.
- FOREVER-VALID: a member checked-in under plexus-2026 BEFORE the carryover still gets their certificate 200 name="Plexus Conference 2026" AFTER 2026 was archived and 2099 became the active edition.
- NO-CARRYOVER regression check (DB restored to the single plexus-2026 edition): /api/conferences/active, schedule and a fresh 2026 attendee's certificate all still resolve plexus-2026 with a PLX26-CERT number — default behavior unchanged.
- Browser (Playwright, member JWT injected into medx_user_token): authenticated member portal desktop 1440x900 AND mobile 390x844 = 0 console errors (the anonymous pre-login load's only errors were expected 401s; the single warning is a pre-existing apple-mobile-web-app-capable deprecation, not from this change). In-page fetch of all 5 routes returned the 2099 edition through the SPA's own token.

COPY: changes are code + comments only; no new member-facing strings (the sole user-facing literal, the default certificate name "Plexus Conference 2026", is retained purely as a fallback). No semicolons-in-copy risk; the banned word "honest" is absent.

CLEANUP: dev DB restored from a pre-test snapshot — every carryover/registration/certificate and the e2e.carryover / e2e.oldmember / e2e.sanity users are gone; the 5 pre-existing e2e.applicant/new1/consider accelerator fixtures stay by rule; orphan test ticket PNGs and the Playwright artifact dir removed. Active conference back to Plexus 2026; both portals left running on the final fixed code. No git add/commit.

## 2026-07-07 — 5a5j EVENT-DAY STAFF TRACKING (admin-portal only; live ops map + opt-in sharing + stale push)

Built the event-day live staff-location feature end-to-end in the ADMIN portal (staff are admins/volunteers, so the sharing UI lives where they actually are). Speakers are never users here, so they can never opt in — they appear only indirectly as the subject of an escort pairing chip.

BACKEND (admin server.js, all OUTSIDE the SCHEMA-MIRROR block — admin-only tables; the only cross-portal hop is the stale PUSH, which rides the EXISTING shared push_outbox that the user portal drains):
- 4 tables: staff_tracking_settings (singleton: active/event_label/ends_at/stale_minutes), staff_tracking_consent (per-user opt-in + consent_at + last_prompt_at), staff_positions (live-only, id/user_id/lat/lng/accuracy/heading/speed/recorded_at, indexed), staff_pairings (staff_user_id ↔ speaker_id|guest_name + note + status).
- Staff endpoints (staffOrAdmin): GET /me, POST /consent {enabled}, POST /ping (fail-closed: no row unless enabled AND window open; trail capped at 40), POST /pairing-confirm.
- Admin endpoints (adminOnly): GET /live (freshness fresh/aging/stale from server clock, movement stationary/moving/driving from device speed or successive-point speed, chip from pairing), pairings CRUD, GET/POST /settings, POST /purge, GET /roster (is_admin|is_staff users + speakers), POST /run-scan.
- 60s sweep in the app.listen block: while an event day is on, PUSHes a one-tap check-in ("Are you still with {name}? ..." / "Where are you? ...") to any opted-in staffer whose latest position is older than stale_minutes (throttled one prompt per stale window; enqueues push_outbox with target_email + url = ADMIN_PORTAL_URL/?track=1). When ends_at passes it hard-deletes every position, disables all consents, and switches the event day off (end-of-day auto-purge). Purge button = same wipe on demand.

FRONTEND (admin index.html): Leaflet 1.9.4 vendored locally at /vendor/leaflet (free OSM tiles, no key). Two self-contained modules appended before </body> that monkey-patch App.showSection/App.showApp (the TeamChat/ReviewEngine pattern) so the giant App object is untouched. StaffShare = global sharing FAB + persistent "Sharing location — {event}" indicator chip + bottom-sheet panel (big opt-in toggle, GDPR consent text, live status, one-tap pairing confirm), watchPosition + 15s heartbeat streaming, visibilitychange keep-alive + pagehide final ping, auto-resume on load if enabled, ?track=1 deep-link opens the panel. StaffTracking = new "Live Ops Map" admin nav section: Leaflet map with colored pins + staleness rings (green/amber/red) + accuracy circles, live roster with freshness dots + movement + escort chips, event-day settings bar (label/ends/stale/turn-on/save/run-scan/purge), pairings CRUD, 4s auto-refresh polling. All EN (admin surface). Inline onclick is safe here (helmet script-src-attr: unsafe-inline).

GDPR: explicit per-person opt-in with a timestamp; disabling stops streaming instantly AND deletes that person's trail; positions visible to admins only; the sharing person always sees their own status; event-day scope with auto-delete at end + manual purge. Fail-closed everywhere.

VERIFIED E2E (Playwright, desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844): opt-in gating (no ping before enable, fail-closed when window closed/consent off), positions land + map marker renders, staleness bands flip fresh→aging→stale, movement "driving · 54 km/h", pairing chip "Escort:/With Prof. Ivan Horvat", stale push enqueues a pairing-aware push_outbox row to the staffer's email (mocked staleness clock) with throttle, manual purge + end-of-day auto-purge both prove ZERO residue (positions/consent/active all wiped, /live returns 0), disable stops streaming instantly (watchId + heartbeat cleared, indicator hidden), non-opted admins never appear (only enabled consent in /live), speakers never appear (not users). Fixed 2 bugs during verify: window.App→bare App (top-level const not on window, wire() never installed), and an inline style="display:none" on the panel that beat the .open CSS class. Only console errors are environmental blocked externals (Google Fonts, chart.js CDN, OSM tiles) — none from this feature; Leaflet is vendored precisely to avoid a CDN dependency. node --check both servers OK, check-schema-sync.sh exit 0. Test junk cleaned. NOT committed (per campaign rules). Optional prod env: ADMIN_PORTAL_URL (defaults to https://medx-admin-portal.onrender.com, already correct).
