## 2026-07-07 — 5b PRESS/MEDIA FIX: unpublish was irreversible + mislabeled state (1 verified major failure)

### FAILURE (verified, admin-only)
- `POST /api/pr/press-releases/:id/unpublish` set `status='draft'` but did NOT clear `published_at` (publish stamps it via `published_at = COALESCE(published_at, ?)`, so it stayed set forever). The list handler `GET /api/pr/press-releases` decided pair status with `if ((r.published_at || '') && ...) status='published'` — so any pair that was EVER published reported `status='published'` even after unpublish. The frontend `pressCardHtml` derives BOTH the chip and the publish/unpublish button from `p.status === 'published'`, so an unpublished (draft) release still showed a green "Published" chip and only an "Unpublish" button (a no-op) — never a "Publish" button. Re-publishing through the UI was impossible; only workaround was delete + recreate. Public was already correct (`/api/public/press[/:slug]` filter `status='published'`), so scope was admin-only → major, not blocker.

### FIX (minimal, one location)
- Unpublish handler (admin-portal/backend/server.js ~L24505) now clears the stamp: `UPDATE press_releases SET status='draft', published_at = NULL, updated_at = ? WHERE id = ?`. This makes `published_at` an accurate current-publish signal, so the list handler reports the true status and the frontend renders the correct chip + button with no frontend change needed. Applied via python3 heredoc unique-string replace (assert count==1). `node --check` OK. No schema change; check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (mirror 435 lines byte-identical). Public ordering unaffected (public filters status and orders by `COALESCE(dateline_date, published_at)`; only drafts — excluded from public — lose the stamp).

### PROVEN E2E (admin restarted by PORT 3002, nohup, /tmp/medx-admin-3002.log)
- API round trip on a fresh EN+HR pair: PUBLISH → DB both `published`+stamped, list `published`, public feed shows 2. UNPUBLISH → DB both `draft` + `published_at` NULL, list `draft`, public feed 0. RE-PUBLISH (was impossible before) → DB both `published`+re-stamped, list `published`, public feed 2.
- Live admin UI (Playwright, login juginovic.alen@gmail.com): the previously-broken card now probes `{chip:"Draft", hasPublishBtn:true, hasUnpublishBtn:false}` (was `{chip:"Published", hasPublishBtn:false, hasUnpublishBtn:true}`). Full UI-button round trip via the real buttons: initial Draft/Publish → click Publish → Published/Unpublish → click Unpublish → Draft/Publish. Desktop 1440 + mobile 390 both correct. Current-navigation console = only the 4 offline-sandbox external CDN/font failures (Google Fonts + jsdelivr chart.js), zero app errors from the flow.
- Test junk cleaned: the E2E press pair deleted via the API delete path (press_releases rows for the pair = 0, no "E2E …" titles remain). Accelerator e2e rows untouched.

### Frozen surfaces untouched
- Stripe/checkout/calculateTotal/POST /api/registrations, QR gen+scanner+member payload, payment/invite links, legacy accelerator — none touched. Only change: one UPDATE statement in the press unpublish handler. No git add/commit. Both dev servers left running (3001 user, 3002 admin).


## 2026-07-07 — 5a5c CME / HLK ACCREDITATION (admin flag + additive physician registration section + consented CSV export)

Built the Croatian Medical Chamber (Hrvatska liječnička komora / HLK) CME-accreditation capability. Per conference edition an admin flags "Accredited by the Croatian Medical Chamber (HLK)" and sets a CME points value + accreditation number; an accredited event ADDITIVELY shows physicians a registration-form section collecting date of birth + OIB with EXPLICIT un-pre-ticked consent and a clear bilingual purpose statement; consented attendees export to CSV for chamber reporting. CME credit is OFFICIAL chamber credit, DECOUPLED from the internal rewards points (zero coupling — no points_ledger writes touch this data). Edition-aware by construction (keyed to conference_id — each Plexus edition is its own conferences row, so a carried-over edition starts un-accredited until re-flagged). FROZEN paths untouched: Stripe/checkout, calculateTotal, POST /api/registrations, /api/plexus/register, QR gen+scanner+member payload — all byte-identical (git: 0 deletions in both backends; the single git "deletion" in the user frontend is the gala.dressCodeVal dict line gaining a trailing comma to chain the new i18n keys — the entry is preserved).

SCHEMA (both server.js, added IDENTICALLY OUTSIDE the SCHEMA-MIRROR block — both portals touch these; check-schema-sync.sh exit 0, 435 lines byte-identical): cme_accreditations (conference_id PK, is_accredited, points_value, accreditation_number, updated_by, updated_at) + cme_submissions (id PK, registration_id UNIQUE, conference_id, user_id, date_of_birth, oib, enc, consent, consent_at, consent_purpose, created/updated_at) + 2 indexes. cme_submissions is the SUPPLEMENTAL table keyed by registration id — the parallel post-submit attach mechanism that keeps the frozen POST untouched.

ENCRYPTION-AT-REST (no prior pattern in the codebase — added one, first-class fallback): AES-256-GCM helpers cmeEncrypt/cmeDecrypt keyed off a new shared CME_ENC_KEY (scrypt-derived 32 bytes), identical top-level helpers in both server.js. DOB + OIB stored as 'gcm:iv:tag:ct' (enc=1) when the key is set; with NO key they fall back to plaintext (enc=0) so the feature never hard-fails; a stored ciphertext with no key returns '[encrypted]' rather than throwing. CME_ENC_KEY added to BOTH dev backend/.env (same value — shared DB). OWNER ACTION: set the SAME CME_ENC_KEY in both Render services for prod at-rest encryption. OIB validated server-side + client-side via ISO 7064 Mod 11,10 (reused the existing PlexusPortal._isValidOIB).

BACKEND — USER (3 endpoints): GET /api/plexus/cme/status (public, benign, drives the form section; never exposes the accreditation number), POST /api/plexus/cme/attach (auth; registration must belong to caller, event must be accredited, consent must be explicit true, DOB required, OIB checksum-valid; upsert by registration_id; records consent_at + consent_purpose; encrypts DOB+OIB; NEVER writes points_ledger), GET /api/plexus/cme/my (auth; member's own decrypted record). BACKEND — ADMIN (4 endpoints): GET /api/admin/cme/events (editions + accreditation + consented/total counts), PUT /api/admin/cme/events/:cid (set flag/points/number; points non-negative), GET .../attendees (consented-only, decrypted, admin), GET .../export.csv (BOM + header + consented-only rows w/ name/email/DOB/OIB/points/accreditation-number/consent-timestamp).

FRONTEND — USER (index.html, 9 additive splices asserted count==1): physician CME section after the accessibility field (hidden by default; loadCMEStatus() reveals it only when the active edition is accredited + fills a points chip), un-pre-ticked consent checkbox gating collection, DOB + OIB fields, EN+HR i18n cluster (formal Vi, no semicolons), pre-submit validation (consent ticked => DOB + valid OIB required; fields filled but unticked => blocked — never collect without explicit consent), _attachCMEIfNeeded() fires right after registration success (before any Stripe redirect) keyed by the returned registration_id, and a MyMedX "Vaši CME bodovi" self-view (renderMyCme) reading /api/plexus/cme/my — shown to quiet-profile members identically (it is official credit, not gamification). FRONTEND — ADMIN (index.html, 5 additive splices): "CME / HLK" nav item after Editions, sectionNames entry, showSection hook, section-cme (scoped CSS + per-edition cards: accredited toggle, points + accreditation-number inputs, consented/total counts, Save / View submissions / Export CSV), and the CmeAdmin module (top-level const + window mirror, inline handlers under the admin CSP). Plus an ASSIST_KB entry ('cme-hlk') so the admin conversational assistant answers "how do I accredit an event for CME" with steps + a deep link to the CME section (spec point 1 parenthetical).

VERIFY (E2E, both servers restarted on final code — kill by PORT, explicit PORT=3001/3002, nohup, /tmp logs, NODE_ENV=development, shared dev DB, CME_ENC_KEY set; minted real admin + member JWTs):

- Browser (Playwright, real JWTs, real inline-handler clicks): ADMIN desktop 1440x900 CME section renders real data; toggled Accredited via the real checkbox (badge -> Accredited, points+number fields appeared), set 5 + HLK-2026-1234, Saved -> DB persisted (updated_by=the real admin email); View submissions showed the decrypted consented row; Export CSV fetched text/csv w/ the row+points+number; empty-state correct; mobile 390x844 no horizontal overflow. MEMBER: section shows when accredited (5 CME points chip) and HIDDEN when un-accredited (real loadCMEStatus + real API); consent unchecked by default; real _isValidOIB accepts valid / rejects invalid; EN + HR (Trajno medicinsko usavršavanje / 5 CME bodova / Datum rođenja / OIB / formal-Vi consent) both rendered; MyMedX self-view rendered the member's own record (decrypted OIB, event, 5 CME bodova, consent date, "separate from rewards points", "visible only to you and admins"). ## 2026-07-07 — A-small FIX: LiDAR/spatial upload type-gate bypass → stored XSS / same-origin HTML hosting (1 verified major)

### ROOT CAUSE (admin-portal/backend/server.js, spatial upload fileFilter ~L18776)
- The gate was `if (okExt.includes(ext) || okMime.includes(file.mimetype)) return cb(null,true)`. Because `application/octet-stream` (the browser default MIME, trivially attacker-set) is in `okMime`, the OR clause admitted ANY extension. The file is then stored keeping its ORIGINAL extension (`path.extname(file.originalname)`) and `/uploads` is served by `express.static` with NO auth — and the stored EXTENSION, not the MIME, governs the served `Content-Type`. So `evil.html` uploaded with `type=application/octet-stream` was stored as `<uuid>.html` and served back UNauthenticated as `Content-Type: text/html` with `<script>` intact, executing on the admin origin (CSP script-src `'self' 'unsafe-inline'` → can read the admin JWT from localStorage). Same class for `.svg`/`.xhtml`/`.js`. Upload requires an authenticated admin (adminOnly), so this is lateral admin→admin persistence, not anonymous — hence major.

### FIX (minimal, one location)
- The MIME can never be a sole pass because the served Content-Type derives from the stored extension. Removed the `okMime` OR-branch and gated strictly on the allow-listed 3D extension: `if (okExt.includes(ext)) return cb(null, true);` (okExt = `.glb`, `.usdz`, `.gltf`). MIME is now irrelevant to the decision; a real GLB still uploads whether the browser sends `model/gltf-binary` OR `application/octet-stream` (extension is the reliable signal), so the legit path is preserved. Applied via python3 heredoc unique-string replace (assert count==1). `node --check` OK. No schema change; check-schema-sync.sh exit 0 (mirror 435 lines byte-identical).

### PROVEN E2E (admin restarted by PORT 3002, nohup, /tmp/medx-admin-3002.log)
- BEFORE (old server): `POST /api/admin/spatial/assets` with `evil.html;type=application/octet-stream` → HTTP 200, stored `<uuid>.html`; fetched that URL with NO auth → HTTP 200, `Content-Type: text/html; charset=UTF-8`, body = `<html><script>alert(1)</script></html>` intact. Confirmed vulnerable.
- AFTER (fixed server): 4 attack vectors all rejected HTTP 400 `"Only 3D scans are accepted here: a .glb, .usdz or .gltf file."` — evil.html+octet-stream (the exact report repro), evil.svg+octet-stream, evil.js+octet-stream, AND evil.html spoofed as `type=model/gltf-binary` (extension gate holds regardless of MIME). Positive controls: good.glb+octet-stream → HTTP 200, good.glb+model/gltf-binary → HTTP 200; the served legit .glb returns `Content-Type: model/gltf-binary` (never text/html). The pre-fix evil.html was removed via the API delete path and now 404s.
- Cleanup: all 3 test uploads deleted through `DELETE /api/admin/spatial/assets/:id` (row + disk file); no residual test files in uploads/spatial; /tmp probes removed. Pre-existing spatial assets untouched. Accelerator e2e rows untouched. Server log shows zero errors across the whole flow.

### Frozen surfaces untouched
- Stripe/checkout/calculateTotal/POST /api/registrations, QR gen+scanner+member payload, payment/invite links, legacy accelerator — none touched. Only change: the spatial upload fileFilter condition. No git add/commit. Both dev servers left running (3001 user, 3002 admin).

Stripe/checkout/calculateTotal/POST /api/registrations//api/plexus/register, QR gen+scanner+member payload — none touched. No schema change anywhere (schema-sync 435 lines byte-identical, both node --check OK). Both dev servers left running (3001 user / 3002 admin).

## 2026-07-08 — R3-3 EVENT-DAY TICKET SUITE — completed + fully verified E2E (23/23 incl. real offline)

The earlier "Round 3 partial salvage" commit (785078b) captured this lane's working tree mid-task: Present-mode boarding pass, on-device ticket cache, per-ticket Google Wallet endpoint + dormant Apple stub, vendored qrcode 1.5.1, sw.js v9, EN+HR strings, ?view=ticket deep link. That snapshot LOOKED complete but offline did NOT actually work yet — this commit adds the two root-cause fixes found during adversarial offline verification:

### ROOT CAUSE 1 — user-portal SW install silently failed since v8 (cross-origin precache vs CSP)
- `ASSETS_TO_CACHE` included `https://fonts.googleapis.com/...css`. The service worker executes under the server's helmet CSP, whose `connect-src` does not include fonts.googleapis.com → `cache.addAll()` REJECTED → install failed → the SW never activated and never controlled a page. The offline app shell therefore never worked on the member portal (Chromium's non-atomic addAll left same-origin entries in the cache, masking the failure). Proven by differential test against the admin SW (same-origin-only precache — activates + controls in ~1s). FIX: precache list is now same-origin only, with a NOTE in sw.js so the fonts URL never returns.

### ROOT CAUSE 2 — service worker registration was nondeterministic (buried dead code)
- The only `navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js')` call lives in `PWA.registerServiceWorker()`, but the whole `PWA`/`initPhase5` block is swallowed INSIDE the body of `ComparativeAnalytics.addComparison()` — a method only referenced in a comment — and it additionally waits for a window 'load' event that can already have fired. Registration therefore happened rarely/racily. FIX: idempotent `navigator.serviceWorker.register('/sw.js')` at the real DOMContentLoaded boot next to `UserPortal.init()`; the legacy path, if it ever runs, attaches its update-banner listener to the same registration harmlessly.

### VERIFIED E2E (Playwright, 23/23 PASS; test JWT for existing dev user ivan.horvat@test.medx.hr — zero rows created, counts unchanged 4 registrations / 34 users)
- SW registers deterministically, activates, precaches shell+vendored qrcode under `medx-portal-v9`, page controlled via clients.claim.
- NO /api/ response in any cache, online or offline (fetch of /api/registrations/my while offline fails — never served from cache).
- Wallet card QR ≥148px; Present + Google Wallet + Apple Wallet buttons; "Saved on this device" note; `medx_ticket_cache` written after fetch.
- Present mode: full-screen boarding pass (guest name, event, ticket type + seat when present, dates/venue, status chip, invoice), QR rendered at 232px from the EXACT frozen payload (`ticket_qr_code || 'MEDX:'+id` — byte-identity asserted vs the wallet card), Escape/close works, wake-lock best-effort.
- Google Wallet per-ticket endpoint returns the clean bilingual key-gate when GOOGLE_WALLET_* env is absent; ownership enforced in SQL (cross-user regId → 404, proven). Apple ticket route wired-but-dormant (owner action: Apple Developer Pass Type ID cert, env names documented in the route).
- EN+HR: "Pokažite ulaznicu" button, HR overlay hint "Pokažite ovaj zaslon pri prijavi.", Gost label — verified rendered in HR locale.
- OFFLINE (context.setOffline(true)): renderer navigation to `/?app=1&view=ticket` (the manifest "My Ticket" shortcut, now actually wired) served from the SW shell cache, auto-opens Present mode, QR ≥148px with real modules, name+event from the on-device cache, offline banner shown. Screenshots byte-comparable online vs offline.
- Playwright gotcha for future lanes: CDP navigations (page.goto/page.reload) BYPASS service-worker interception in this Chromium — offline navigation tests must use renderer-initiated `location.assign(...)`, and the page must be claim-controlled first.

### Frozen surfaces untouched
- QR payload generation, scanner, member payload, Stripe/checkout, calculateTotal, POST /api/registrations, /api/plexus/register — none modified. The QR library is the same pinned qrcode@1.5.1 build, now self-hosted for offline; payloads byte-identical.

## 2026-07-09 — 5a5j EVENT-DAY STAFF TRACKING — shipped end-to-end + verified on the REAL Render deployment (25/25 PASS, production DB, zero residue)

The engine shipped in af30983 (QW8) and was dev-verified there; this lane closed the loop to a live, reachable, verified feature. Two ship-blockers found and fixed first: (e8d7a8a) 'event-tracking' joined OPS_ALWAYS_VISIBLE — it was in neither that list nor the onboarding sectionDefs, so the Live Ops Map nav was display:none for every already-onboarded admin including the owner (the a0d16bf auctions/editions defect class), and the streams-while-open constraint is now stated in the UI itself (staff consent panel + admin roster empty-state), not only in code comments. Then (575051a) a REAL production defect found live: the Live Ops Map rendered grey — 0/9 OSM tiles painted — because both service workers intercept cross-origin GETs and re-issue them from worker scope, where the worker's own CSP connect-src (self + Stripe/Cloudinary/Resend only) blocks them; the handler's .catch(() => null) turned that into net::ERR_FAILED. Proven by A/B on prod (SW blocked = 9/9 tiles, SW active = 0/9), fixed with one guard in both workers (never intercept cross-origin; the browser fetches natively under the page's img-src https:, and the type==='basic' cache gate never stored cross-origin responses anyway). The same fix un-breaks Google Fonts + CDN assets for members whenever the user-portal SW controls the page — the prior lanes' "environmental" console entries were partly this real bug. stamp-sw.sh cache-busts both workers per deploy, so clients converge on next load.

## 2026-07-10 — ADMIN HOME CALM-DOWN: live verification + 3 fixes (00078fc proven on Render via 194cd4c + c2191a0 + 8c107ad, zero prod residue)

An orchestrated verify lane flagged "admin-home-calm" but delivered an empty failure list (uninterpolated template — the auction-lane precedent), so this lane re-verified 00078fc independently on https://medx-admin-portal.onrender.com with the owner's production admin account, real form login + real clicks (Playwright chromium, desktop 1440x900 + mobile 390x844). Three real defects found and fixed at the root, each committed + pushed + redeploy-polled separately:

(194cd4c) /api/app-install-qr.png was DEAD CODE in production — 41e67aa registered it AFTER app.use('/api', …) 404 catch-all, so prod returned {"error":"API endpoint not found"} and the Resources team-app QR modal + print sheet showed a broken image. Pure move of the identical handler above the catch-all, with a comment so the next late route does not repeat this. Proven locally first (200 image/png 480x480; QR decodes to https://medx-admin-portal.onrender.com — a URL QR, byte-unrelated to the frozen ticket-QR payload; unknown /api/* still 404 JSON).

(c2191a0) ONE-RED STRAGGLERS — a computed-style scan of every visible element on the live home + sidebar found legacy reds the calm-down missed: Swiss-clock second hand (#ef4444), clock-timer warning + timer-cancel + unpin-hover family (#ef4444), "New Diaspora Link" quick-action globe (inline #ef4444), ContentChecklist "Website" area tag (#c14b52, rendered 7x in the checklist card), and the location-sharing indicator/FAB (#dc2626). All folded into var(--danger) / rgba(var(--red-rgb), a); the Website tag takes the crimson hex literal because the renderer composes alpha as ${color}22. Hidden non-home sections deliberately untouched.

(8c107ad) QR-modal Copy link leaked an unhandled NotAllowedError pageerror when the clipboard is blocked (writeText promise inside a sync try/catch) while still toasting "Link copied." — now the promise is handled, success toasts only on success, blocked copy points to the link shown in the modal.

(big gold arrow, "Registration" / "Registracija"). ZERO prod residue — every generator is

## 2026-07-22 — GAME DAY MODE: focused event-day dashboard, ops chat, volunteer magic-link invites — 0a79740 + 9a29c25 (branch gameday-mode, PR pending)

Replaces the event-day WhatsApp sprawl with ONE in-portal surface. A mode setting (off |
auto | test) resolves ONE live event — auto activates for whichever event's date window
covers today in Europe/Zagreb (conferences.start_date/end_date + gala_settings.date), test
pins an event for rehearsals. While active, entering the admin portal lands EVERYONE on a
game-day dashboard scoped to that event only; core admins escape with one "Puni portal /
Full portal" button (sessionStorage, so reloads mid-work don't re-trap); scanner staff land
there too (endpoints are staffOrAdmin). Magic-link volunteers (?gd=TOKEN) get a separate
DOWNGRADED session (JWT kind 'gameday_volunteer', no users.id, invite row re-checked per
request so Revoke kills live sessions within one poll) and are LOCKED to the game-day view
— sidebar and all admin chrome hidden, never finances, never other events.

### Schema (admin-only, guarded, OUTSIDE the SCHEMA-MIRROR block — admin server.js only)
- gameday_settings — one row (CHECK id=1): mode off|auto|test, test_event_key, updated_by/at.
- gameday_messages — ops chat; channel is a FIXED key ('glavni' + organizacija, registracija,
  tehnika, catering, security, volonteri), rowid is the polling cursor, indexed on
  (channel, created_at).
- gameday_invites — token UNIQUE (24 random bytes hex), name/email/team, grants_json
  (per-volunteer permissions checklist: registrations-view, program-view, speakers-view,
  meals-view, recent-checkins, team-roster), last_seen_at presence stamp, revoked.

### Backend (admin server.js, all after SCHEMA-MIRROR:END, registered BEFORE the /api 404 catch-all)
/api/admin/gameday/{status,settings GET/PUT,dashboard,messages GET/POST,invites CRUD +
revoke} (status/dashboard/chat staffOrAdmin, the rest adminOnly, all audited) and
/api/gameday/volunteer/{login,status,dashboard,messages GET/POST,checkin} (login limiter
30/min/IP; data limiter 120/min keyed PER INVITE, not per IP — the whole crew polls chat
from one venue Wi-Fi). The dashboard aggregate reuses the EXISTING check-in columns
(registrations.checked_in / gala_registrations + payment gate) and the sessions day-offset
/ gala schedule_json, so numbers always agree with the frozen scanner. The volunteer door
scan is a separate ADDITIVE endpoint mirroring /api/admin/checkin/verify semantics
(uuid / JSON-v2 QR payload / short-code prefix / email, gala not_paid refusal) and writing
the same checked_in columns — the frozen Stripe/checkout, POST /api/registrations, QR
generation and scanner internals are byte-untouched.

### Frontend (index.html, all additive)
section-gameday + section-gameday-settings, "Game Day" nav item (Events & access) with a
pulsing live dot, GameDay/GameDaySettings modules wired via the wireDiscover-style
showSection decorator. Dashboard is HR-first (gala-tooling register): Dan X/Y pill,
countdown to the next programme item, SADA/SLJEDEĆE schedule highlighting, scanned/expected
stat tiles, 4s polling chat with team rooms (volunteers see glavni + their own room only),
team roster with online dots, quick links (skener / raspored stolova + tlocrt via the
existing plexus-tables console / door list), grant-gated cards. Dashboard polls 12s; card
repaints SKIP while an input is focused (door staff never lose a half-typed code) and the
last scan result survives repaints. Settings chrome stays English per admin convention.

### Verified E2E (local boot exactly like CI: NODE_ENV=test, scratch db, port 3102)
Playwright, 34/34: admin lands on gameday when auto-active, escape + no re-trap on reload,
off/test/auto toggling flips the nav dot, invite created with grants via UI, volunteer
magic link in a fresh context → locked view + name pre-set + granted cards only + scoped
channels, cross-context polling delivery both directions, volunteer door scan (new + dupe),
revoke → lockout screen on next poll, wrong token rejected, mobile 390w no horizontal
scroll. API-level: volunteer JWT 401s on admin routes, grant/channel scoping, uuid/JSON-v2/
short-code scans, gala not_paid refusal. Gates: node --check both servers, check-schema-sync
512 lines byte-identical, check-api-contract OK, 8/8 inline script blocks parse, CI boot
trio (/ 200, /health 200, bad-creds login 401). Evidence (8 screenshots desktop+mobile):
~/Documents/Claude_Code_Projects/MedX/MedX_GameDay_2026-07-22/.

## 2026-07-22 — ACCOUNT LINKING + TICKET WALLET COMPLETENESS — 9fe2f40 (branch account-linking, PR #36)

Guests keep registering via the public forms with no account; members get "everything in
one place". (1) Optional member-login card on the public forms — SPA gala form + Plexus
wizard reuse the existing auth modal (MemberLinkCard, flips to "Prijavljeni ste kao …"
after login and prefills); the server-rendered /plexus, /building-bridges and /donor-night
pages show a signed-in banner + prefill from the portal localStorage login and send the
JWT. /api/gala/register, /api/public-events/register and /api/croatians-abroad/register
take optionalAuth and stamp user_id; the anonymous path is untouched (invalid/absent
token still registers, user_id NULL). (2) Retroactive claiming —
claimRegistrationsForUser() on signup/login/email-verify attaches guest rows across
registrations / gala_registrations / bridges_registrations /
croatians_abroad_registrations by lower(email) WHERE user_id IS NULL, plus a one-time
drip_log-guarded backfill (kind account_claim_backfill_v1) for all existing users.
GOTCHA fixed en route: db.run() WITHOUT params goes through exec(), so the
getRowsModified() polyfill reads 0 — count before update for unparameterized DML.
(3) Wallet — new GET /api/gala/my feeds gala ticket cards in My Med&X (status chip incl.
pending approval, gala_settings date/venue, hosted frozen /qr/:id.png). (4) Stol N —
NEW email-keyed gala_table_assignments INSIDE the SCHEMA-MIRROR block in BOTH server.js
(user reads, admin writes; 524 lines byte-identical); admin Gala → Seating "Uvezi stolove
iz konzole (CSV)" card upserts the picker console's exported CSV by lower(email)
(re-import updates, audited) — Firestore console stays decoupled; /api/gala/my-seat
prefers the console import, falls back to the in-portal seating plan. Google Wallet
verdict: REAL signed savetowallet JWT implementation, needs owner env
(GOOGLE_WALLET_ISSUER_ID + GOOGLE_WALLET_SA_KEY) — until then a clean bilingual gate.

### Verified E2E (local boot exactly like CI: NODE_ENV=test, scratch db, 3101/3102)
API 33/33: anonymous paths user_id NULL, logged-in submits linked, claim on signup
(mixed-case email) + on login, CSV import insert/skip + case-insensitive upsert on
re-import, my-seat "Stol 15", Google Wallet gate; DB-level: backfill attaches legacy
NULL rows with true counts, guard blocks re-runs. Playwright 18/18: card → auth modal →
linked state + prefill, wallet desktop + mobile 390w (conference + gala + Stol 15, no
h-scroll, no page errors), donor-night banner, admin import via UI. Gates: node --check
both servers, check-schema-sync 524 lines byte-identical, check-api-contract OK, 3/3
public-page inline script blocks parse, CI boot trio. Evidence (7 screenshots + test
scripts): ~/Documents/Claude_Code_Projects/MedX/MedX_AccountLinking_2026-07-22/.
